| `\text{NG-G1}\text{–}\text{NG-G5}` | §42 列出的五個反向蘊含，各自明確標記為 FALSE 或 OPEN，防止本輪的正向結果被誤讀成更強的等價或可逆結論。 | The five reverse implications listed in §42, each explicitly flagged FALSE or OPEN, blocking any misreading of this round's forward results as stronger equivalences or reversible conclusions. | C4-G |
| `\text{G-CROSSFUNNEL, G-RELAYGEOM, G-OPRATIO, G-HDER, G-V4, G-SWEEP}` | §43 為 X_Integral_Unified_Program 新增的六條守則，要求未來各輪保存本輪建立的共同前提、幾何憑證與分支界線，不得任意升格。 | The six guard clauses added to the X_Integral_Unified_Program in §43, requiring future rounds to preserve this round's shared antecedents, geometric certificates, and branch boundaries without unwarranted upgrades. | C4-G |
| `\text{C4-H}` | §41 提出、§46 正式命名的下一輪主題，處理 Miller Ratio、Middle-Strain 幾何、壓力與導數鏈之間的算子到 regularity-gate 封閉問題，§47 列出 H1–H8 八項證明義務。 | The next round's theme first raised in §41 and formally named in §46, addressing the operator-to-regularity-gate closure among the Miller ratio, middle-strain geometry, pressure, and derivative chains, with eight proof obligations H1-H8 listed in §47. | C4-G |

## C4-I

| 符號 Symbol | 中文定義 | Definition (EN) | 出處 Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `J_j=(\tau_j,\tau_{j+1}),\ J=(a,b)` | 承接C4-H的收縮式late-time記錄窗口J_j=(tau_j,tau_{j+1})（見§0），本輪§2將其寫成一般記法J=(a,b)作為容量-重疊分析的基本區間。 | The shrinking late-time record windows J_j=(tau_j,tau_{j+1}) inherited from C4-H (§0) are written generically as J=(a,b) in §2, the base interval for this round's capacity-overlap analysis. | C4-I |
| `m(t)=\int_{\mathbb R^3}\lambda_2^+(x,t)\|S(x,t)\|^2\,dx` | §2定義的時間函數，把Miller中間特徵值正部λ_2^+與應變張量平方在空間上積分成單一非負的middle toll密度。 | A time-dependent quantity defined in §2 that integrates the positive part of Miller's middle eigenvalue λ2+ weighted by \|S\|^2 over space into a single nonnegative middle-toll density. | C4-I |
| `o(t)=\nu[\zeta(t)r_\nu(t)-1]_+\\|\Delta S(t)\\|_2^2` | §2定義的時間函數，用C4-H的閾值超額[ζr_ν-1]_+乘上‖ΔS‖²_2衡量算子項超出增長閾值的負荷。 | A time-dependent quantity defined in §2 measuring how far the operator term exceeds the growth threshold, via [ζ r_ν - 1]_+ times the squared norm of ΔS. | C4-I |
| `M=\operatorname*{ess\,sup}_{t\in J}m(t),\quad O=\operatorname*{ess\,sup}_{t\in J}o(t)` | §3將m、o在記錄窗口J上的本質上確界分別記作M、O，作為之後duty-cycle下界公式的分母。 | §3 sets M and O as the essential suprema of m and o over the record window J, the denominators in the duty-cycle lower bounds that follow. | C4-I |
| `E_m(\mu)=\{t\in J:m(t)\ge\mu\},\quad E_o(\omega)=\{t\in J:o(t)\ge\omega\}` | §3定義的時間子集，分別收集middle load與operator load超過給定閾值μ、ω的時刻。 | Time subsets defined in §3 collecting the moments at which the middle load and operator load exceed given thresholds μ and ω respectively. | C4-I |
| `\|E_m(\mu)\cap E_o(\omega)\|\ge\left[\frac{A-\mu\|J\|}{M-\mu}+\frac{B-\omega\|J\|}{O-\omega}-\|J\|\right]_+` | §5定理5.1，由包含-排斥不等式從單通道duty-cycle下界推出E_m(μ)、E_o(ω)的same-time重疊測度下界，是本輪核心正面結果。 | Theorem 5.1 in §5, deriving a lower bound on the measure of the same-time overlap E_m(μ)∩E_o(ω) from single-channel duty-cycle bounds via inclusion-exclusion — the round's central positive result. | C4-I |
| `\text{Middle–Operator Peak-Capacity Desynchronization Debt}` | §8將E_m(μ)∩E_o(ω)=∅時必然成立的容量比不等式命名為此債務，量化若無同時重疊峰值容量比需付出的代價。 | §8 names the capacity-ratio inequality that must hold whenever E_m(μ)∩E_o(ω)=∅ as this debt, quantifying the cost paid in peak-capacity ratios when same-time overlap fails. | C4-I |
| `M\le K_m\frac{A}{\|J\|},\ O\le K_o\frac{B}{\|J\|}\ \Rightarrow\ \tfrac1{K_m}+\tfrac1{K_o}>1\Rightarrow\text{overlap forced}` | §10的條件式定理：若峰值容量M、O被平均容量的常數倍K_m、K_o控制且1/K_m+1/K_o>1，則same-time overlap被迫成立，但目前未有已證的一致K_m、K_o上界。 | The conditional theorem of §10: if peak capacities M, O are bounded by constant multiples K_m, K_o of the averages and 1/K_m+1/K_o>1, same-time overlap is forced, though no uniform bound on K_m, K_o is yet proved. | C4-I |
| `D=\\|\Delta S\\|_2,\qquad e_D=\frac{-\Delta S}{D}` | §12定義的‖ΔS‖_2模長D及對應單位方向e_D=-ΔS/D，作為之後算子平行/正交分解的參考軸。 | The magnitude D=‖ΔS‖_2 and its associated unit vector e_D=-ΔS/D, defined in §12 as the reference axis for the operator's parallel/orthogonal decomposition that follows. | C4-I |
| `\widehat Q=\frac{\mathcal Q_{SV}}{\nu D},\qquad \\|\widehat Q\\|_2=r_\nu` | §12把Miller strain/vorticity算子Q_SV（引自§1.2）除以νD正規化為Q̂，其模長即為C4-H沿用的Miller比r_ν。 | §12 normalizes Miller's strain/vorticity operator Q_SV (introduced in §1.2) by νD into Q̂, whose norm recovers the Miller ratio r_ν carried over from C4-H. | C4-I |
| `g=\zeta r_\nu,\quad \widehat Q=-g\,e_D+Q_\perp,\quad \tfrac12\tfrac{d}{dt}\\|S\\|_{\dot H^1}^2=\nu(g-1)D^2` | §13將Q̂沿e_D的投影係數命名為g=ζr_ν，並證明Ḣ¹增長率恰為ν(g-1)D²，使g取代單純算子範數成為§46所稱的真正gate variable。 | §13 names the projection coefficient of Q̂ along e_D as g=ζr_ν and shows the Ḣ¹ growth rate equals ν(g-1)D², making g — rather than the bare operator norm — the round's true gate variable, as stated explicitly in §46. | C4-I |
| `Q_\perp,\qquad \\|Q_\perp\\|_2^2=r_\nu^2-g^2` | §13由畢氏定理得到的Q̂正交於e_D的殘餘分量，其模長平方等於r_ν²-g²。 | The component of Q̂ orthogonal to e_D obtained via the Pythagorean identity in §13, with squared norm r_ν²-g². | C4-I |
| `r_\nu\ge R>1,\ g\le1\ \Rightarrow\ (g<-1)\ \vee\ \big(-1\le g\le1\ \wedge\ \\|Q_\perp\\|_2\ge\sqrt{R^2-1}\big)` | §14定理，證明若Miller比大但g不大於1，則必落入I-OPPOSE（強烈對抗增長，g<-1）或I-ORTH（正交算子分量至少√(R²-1)）之一。 | The theorem of §14 proving that a large Miller ratio with g≤1 must fall into either I-OPPOSE (strongly growth-opposing, g<-1) or I-ORTH (orthogonal operator component at least √(R²-1)). | C4-I |
| `W=P_{st}(\omega\otimes\omega),\qquad \langle W,-\Delta S\rangle=0,\qquad W\in\{e_D\}^\perp` | §16定義W=P_st(ω⊗ω)，並引用Miller正交性⟨W,-ΔS⟩=0證明它恆屬於增長方向的正交補空間。 | §16 defines W=P_st(ω⊗ω) and invokes Miller's orthogonality ⟨W,-ΔS⟩=0 to show it always lies in the orthogonal complement of the growth direction. | C4-I |
| `A=P_{st}\big((u\cdot\nabla)S+S^2\big),\qquad \mathcal Q_{SV}=A+\tfrac34W,\qquad \langle A,e_D\rangle=\langle\mathcal Q_{SV},e_D\rangle` | §17定義A=P_st((u·∇)S+S²)，證明所有growth-parallel資訊都活在A中，並給出Miller算子的分解Q_SV=A+¾W。 | §17 defines A=P_st((u·∇)S+S²), shows all growth-parallel information lives in A, and gives the decomposition Q_SV=A+(3/4)W of Miller's operator. | C4-I |
| `\\|(\mathcal Q_{SV})_\perp\\|_2\ge Q_0\ \Rightarrow\ \\|A_\perp\\|_2\ge\tfrac{Q_0}2\ \vee\ \\|W\\|_2\ge\tfrac{2Q_0}3` | §18定理，證明growth-orthogonal算子壅塞(Q_SV)_⊥若夠大，必由正交平流/應變平方分量A_⊥或渦度平方項W之一承擔。 | The theorem of §18 showing that if the growth-orthogonal operator congestion (Q_SV)_⊥ is large enough, it must be carried by either the orthogonal advection/strain-square component A_⊥ or the vorticity-quadratic term W. | C4-I |
| `g>1\ \Rightarrow\ \big(-\langle A_{adv},-\Delta S\rangle>\tfrac{\nu D^2}2\big)\ \vee\ \big(-\langle A_{S^2},-\Delta S\rangle>\tfrac{\nu D^2}2\big)` | §19-20定理，把A分成平流部分A_adv與應變平方部分A_S²，證明g>1時增長必至少一半來自I-ADV或I-SSA其中之一，渦度項因Miller正交性不能直接驅動增長。 | The theorem of §19-20 splitting A into an advective part A_adv and a strain-square part A_S², proving that when g>1 the growth must come at least half from I-ADV or I-SSA, since Miller orthogonality forbids the vorticity term from directly driving growth. | C4-I |
| `S=\operatorname{diag}(-2,-1,3),\ \lambda_2(S)=-1<0,\ B=e_1\otimes e_1,\ -\operatorname{tr}(SB^2)=2>0` | §22用具體對角矩陣構造pointwise矩陣代數反例，證明strain-square-aligned的Ḣ¹增長本身不能推出同一點λ_2^+>0，即NG-I3。 | §22 constructs a pointwise matrix-algebra counterexample with an explicit diagonal matrix, showing that strain-square-aligned Ḣ¹ growth alone cannot force λ2+>0 at the same point — logged as NG-I3. | C4-I |
| `\partial_t\chi+u\cdot\nabla\chi+\nu\Delta\chi=0` | §26引入滿足反向平流-擴散方程的局部測試函數χ，作為之後adjoint local core上pressure re-entry分析的權重。 | §26 introduces the local test function χ solving the backward advection-diffusion equation, used as the weight for the pressure-re-entry analysis on the adjoint local core that follows. | C4-I |
| `M_\chi(t)=\int\chi S\,dx,\quad M_\chi'=-B_\chi-P_\chi,\quad B_\chi=\int\chi\Big[S^2+\tfrac14\omega\otimes\omega-\tfrac14\|\omega\|^2I\Big]dx,\quad P_\chi=\int\chi\nabla^2p\,dx` | §26給出C3-U的exact恆等式M_χ'=-B_χ-P_χ，將局部平均應變的時間變化率精確分解成二次平均強迫B_χ與壓力平均強迫P_χ兩項。 | §26 states C3-U's exact identity M_χ'=-B_χ-P_χ, exactly splitting the time-derivative of the local mean strain into a quadratic mean forcing term B_χ and a pressure mean forcing term P_χ. | C4-I |
| `b_\chi=\tfrac{R}{\nu^2}\|B_\chi\|,\quad r_\chi=\tfrac{R}{\nu^2}\|M_\chi'\|,\quad \pi_\chi=\tfrac{R}{\nu^2}\|P_\chi\|,\quad b_\chi\le r_\chi+\pi_\chi` | §27把B_χ、M_χ'、P_χ依半徑R與ν²正規化為無量綱量b_χ、r_χ、π_χ，並給出exact三角不等式b_χ≤r_χ+π_χ。 | §27 normalizes B_χ, M_χ', P_χ by radius R and ν² into the dimensionless quantities b_χ, r_χ, π_χ, and states the exact triangle inequality b_χ≤r_χ+π_χ. | C4-I |
| `b_\chi\ge b_0\ \Rightarrow\ (r_\chi\ge\theta b_0)\ \vee\ (\pi_\chi\ge(1-\theta)b_0)` | §28定理，若二次平均強迫b_χ不小於b_0，則必至少走I-MROT（平均旋轉r_χ夠大）或I-PRESS（壓力強迫π_χ夠大）之一。 | The theorem of §28: if the quadratic mean forcing b_χ is at least b_0, then either I-MROT (mean rotation r_χ large enough) or I-PRESS (pressure forcing π_χ large enough) must hold. | C4-I |
| `\Pi_R^{(2)}=\frac1{\nu^2}\inf_{\ell\in\mathcal A_1}\\|p-\ell\\|_{L^{3/2}(B_{CR})},\qquad \Pi_R^{(2)}\ge c\,\pi_\chi` | §30由§29的Hessian估計定義的正規化臨界L^{3/2}壓力振盪量，滿足Π_R^(2)≥cπ_χ，是本輪連接local pressure Hessian強迫與critical積分範數的橋樑。 | Defined in §30 from the Hessian estimate of §29 as the normalized critical L^{3/2} local pressure oscillation, satisfying Π_R^(2)≥cπ_χ — this round's bridge between local pressure-Hessian forcing and a critical integral norm. | C4-I |
| `b_\chi\ge b_0,\ r_\chi\le\varepsilon<b_0\ \Rightarrow\ \Pi_R^{(2)}\ge c(b_0-\varepsilon)` | §31定理，證明當二次強迫nondegenerate而局部平均應變旋轉被壓低到ε以下時，臨界壓力振盪必至少為c(b_0-ε)。 | The theorem of §31 proving that when the quadratic forcing is nondegenerate but the local mean-strain rotation is suppressed below ε, the critical pressure oscillation must be at least c(b_0-ε). | C4-I |
| `R_n\to0,\ \Pi_{R_n}^{(2)}\ge\pi_0>0\ \Rightarrow\ \int_{B_{CR_n}}\|p\|^{3/2}dx\ge c\,\pi_0^{3/2}\nu^3` | §32命名的結果，證明若收縮核上臨界壓力振盪保持≥π_0，則即使球體體積\|B_CR_n\|→0，對應的L^{3/2}壓力質量仍不消失。 | The result named in §32, showing that if the critical pressure oscillation on shrinking cores stays ≥π_0, the corresponding L^{3/2} pressure mass does not vanish even as the ball volume tends to zero. | C4-I |
| `A_\chi^{quad}=\int\chi\Big\|S^2+\tfrac14\omega\otimes\omega-\tfrac14\|\omega\|^2I\Big\|dx,\qquad a_\chi^{quad}=\tfrac{R}{\nu^2}A_\chi^{quad}` | §33定義，把B_χ被積式取絕對值再積分得到A_χ^quad，並給出正規化版本a_χ^quad，用以和signed平均B_χ對比。 | Defined in §33 by taking the absolute value of B_χ's integrand before integrating to get A_χ^quad, with its normalized version a_χ^quad, contrasted against the signed mean B_χ. | C4-I |
| `\kappa_\chi^{quad}=\frac{\|B_\chi\|}{A_\chi^{quad}}\in[0,1]` | §34定義的比值，衡量signed平均強迫B_χ相對其絕對強度A_χ^quad保留了多少相干性，越接近0代表matrix/spatial cancellation越嚴重。 | The ratio defined in §34 measuring how much of the signed mean forcing B_χ survives relative to its absolute intensity A_χ^quad — values near 0 indicate severe matrix/spatial cancellation. | C4-I |
| `a_\chi^{quad}\ge a_0\ \Rightarrow\ (\kappa_\chi^{quad}<\kappa_0)\ \vee\ (r_\chi\ge\theta\kappa_0a_0)\ \vee\ (\Pi_R^{(2)}\ge c(1-\theta)\kappa_0a_0)` | §35本輪最終定理，證明局部二次絕對強度夠大時，必至少走I-QCANCEL（相干比小）、I-MROT（平均旋轉大）、I-PRESS（臨界壓力振盪大）三者之一。 | The round's culminating theorem in §35, proving that when the local quadratic absolute intensity is large enough, at least one of I-QCANCEL (low coherence), I-MROT (large mean rotation), or I-PRESS (large critical pressure oscillation) must hold. | C4-I |
| `\text{I-D1: Temporal Gate Pulse Separation}\quad\vee\quad\text{I-D2: Local Compensation Separation}` | §44總結C4-I後仍可逃避更強同步化的兩條路徑：I-D1時間脈衝分離（middle/operator在同一J_j內不同次時段支付）與I-D2局部補償分離（透過平均旋轉或二次矩陣消去避開壓力重新進場）。 | §44 summarizes the two remaining escape routes from stronger synchronization after C4-I: I-D1, temporal pulse separation (middle and operator growth paid at different sub-times within the same J_j), and I-D2, local compensation separation (evading pressure re-entry via mean rotation or quadratic matrix cancellation). | C4-I |
| `\Theta_J^{MO}=\langle A,B,M,O,E_m,E_o,\mathfrak C_{overlap}\rangle,\ \Theta^{angle}=\langle r_\nu,g,Q_\perp,A_\perp,W\rangle,\ \Theta_\chi^{reentry}=\langle a_\chi^{quad},\kappa_\chi^{quad},r_\chi,\Pi_R^{(2)}\rangle` | §50為本輪三個子系統各登記一個狀態元組：記錄窗口的Θ_J^MO、算子角度的Θ^angle、與局部壓力重新進場的Θ_χ^reentry，作為True ETN（無限維張力場）框架下的bookkeeping更新。 | §50 registers one state tuple for each of the round's three subsystems — the record-window state Θ_J^MO, the operator-angle state Θ^angle, and the local pressure-reentry state Θ_χ^reentry — as this round's bookkeeping update to the True ETN (infinite-dimensional tension field) framework. | C4-I |

## C4-J

| 符號 Symbol | 中文定義 | Definition (EN) | 出處 Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `\lambda_2^+` | 應變張量三個特徵值中之中間特徵值的正部，第1.1節重申其scale-critical可積性失效是finite-time blow-up的必要正則性閘門（Miller判準）。 | The positive part of the middle eigenvalue of the strain tensor; Section 1.1 reaffirms that its failure of scale-critical integrability is a necessary regularity gate for finite-time blow-up (Miller's criterion). | C4-J |
| `\mathcal Q_{SV}` | 第1.2節引用的算子 $\mathcal Q_{SV}=P_{st}\left((u\cdot\nabla)S+S^2+\frac34\omega\otimes\omega\right)$，連同恆等式 $\langle-\Delta S,\omega\otimes\omega\rangle=0$ 共同刻畫blow-up必逃離之perturbative operator regime。 | The operator $\mathcal Q_{SV}=P_{st}\left((u\cdot\nabla)S+S^2+\frac34\omega\otimes\omega\right)$ cited in Section 1.2, which together with the identity $\langle-\Delta S,\omega\otimes\omega\rangle=0$ characterizes the perturbative operator regime any blow-up must escape. | C4-J |
| `J_j=(\tau_j,\tau_{j+1})` | 第2節沿C4-H/I記錄梯次定義的收縮時間區間，滿足 $\|J_j\|\to0$，是本輪所有compensation ledger的基本時間單元。 | The shrinking time interval along the C4-H/I record ladder introduced in Section 2, satisfying $\|J_j\|\to0$, serving as the basic temporal unit for every compensation ledger in this round. | C4-J |
| `m_j(t)=\int\lambda_2^+\|S\|^2dx` | 第2節定義為 $\int\lambda_2^+\|S\|^2dx$，其在窗口 $J_j$ 上的積分下界為 $A_j>0$。 | Defined in Section 2 as $\int\lambda_2^+\|S\|^2dx$, whose integral over $J_j$ is bounded below by $A_j>0$. | C4-J |
| `o_j(t)=\nu[\zeta r_\nu-1]_+\\|\Delta S\\|_2^2` | 第2節定義為 $\nu[\zeta r_\nu-1]_+\\|\Delta S\\|_2^2$，其在窗口 $J_j$ 上的積分下界為 $B_j>0$。 | Defined in Section 2 as $\nu[\zeta r_\nu-1]_+\\|\Delta S\\|_2^2$, whose integral over $J_j$ is bounded below by $B_j>0$. | C4-J |
| `A_j,\ B_j,\ M_j,\ O_j` | 第2-3節定義的輔助量：$m_j,o_j$ 的積分下界 $A_j,B_j$ 與 $L^\infty(J_j)$ 峰值 $M_j,O_j$，共同組成capacity ratios的分子分母。 | The auxiliary quantities from Sections 2-3: the integral lower bounds $A_j,B_j$ of $m_j,o_j$ and their $L^\infty(J_j)$ peak values $M_j,O_j$, together forming the numerators and denominators of the capacity ratios. | C4-J |
| `K_{m,j},\ K_{o,j}` | 第3節定義的比值，恆滿足 $K_{m,j},K_{o,j}\ge1$，是本輪判定middle-strain與operator-growth兩pulse是否重疊的核心量。 | The ratios defined in Section 3, always satisfying $K_{m,j},K_{o,j}\ge1$, the central quantities governing whether the middle-strain and operator-growth pulses overlap in this round. | C4-J |
| `\frac{1}{K_{m,j}}+\frac{1}{K_{o,j}}>1` | 第4節回顧的C4-I條件，保證zero threshold下 $\{m_j>0\}\cap\{o_j>0\}$ 測度非零，即middle strain與operator growth同時發生。 | The C4-I condition recalled in Section 4, guaranteeing nonzero measure for $\{m_j>0\}\cap\{o_j>0\}$ at the zero threshold, i.e. simultaneous middle-strain and operator-growth occurrence. | C4-J |
| `K_{m,j}=K_{o,j}=2` | 第5節定理5.1，以equal-halves顯式構造證明即使 $K_{m,j}=K_{o,j}=2$ 對所有 $j$ 成立，$m_j,o_j$ 仍可在 $J_j$ 上幾乎處處互斥。 | Theorem 5.1 of Section 5, using an explicit equal-halves construction to show that even with $K_{m,j}=K_{o,j}=2$ for every $j$, $m_j$ and $o_j$ can remain a.e. mutually exclusive on $J_j$. | C4-J |
| `h(t)=\nu(\zeta r_\nu-1)\\|\Delta S\\|_2^2` | 第7節定義為 $\nu(\zeta r_\nu-1)\\|\Delta S\\|_2^2$，恰為應變 $\dot H^1$ 能量 $E_1$ 的時間導數。 | Defined in Section 7 as $\nu(\zeta r_\nu-1)\\|\Delta S\\|_2^2$, exactly the time derivative of the strain $\dot H^1$ energy $E_1$. | C4-J |
| `E_1=\frac12\\|S\\|_{\dot H^1}^2` | 第7節定義為 $\frac12\\|S\\|_{\dot H^1}^2$，其在窗口間的增量 $\Delta E_{1,j}$ 是C4-J.2成長補償恆等式的核心。 | Defined in Section 7 as $\frac12\\|S\\|_{\dot H^1}^2$; its inter-window increment $\Delta E_{1,j}$ is central to the C4-J.2 growth-compensation identity. | C4-J |
| `P_j=\int_{J_j}[h]_+dt,\ N_j=\int_{J_j}[-h]_+dt` | 第7節定義的窗口內成長對齊算子正向支付量 $P_j$ 與反向（over-dissipation）支付量 $N_j$。 | The within-window positive payment $P_j$ and opposing/over-dissipative payment $N_j$ of the growth-aligned operator, defined in Section 7. | C4-J |
| `P_j=\Delta E_{1,j}+N_j` | 第8節定理8.1證明 $P_j-N_j=\Delta E_{1,j}=E_1(\tau_{j+1})-E_1(\tau_j)>0$，說明任何growth-opposing pulse都不是免費的，必增加須支付的正成長變化量。 | Theorem 8.1 of Section 8 proves $P_j-N_j=\Delta E_{1,j}=E_1(\tau_{j+1})-E_1(\tau_j)>0$, showing that any growth-opposing pulse is never free and forces additional positive growth-aligned variation. | C4-J |
| `g(t)=\zeta r_\nu` | 第9、11節沿用之純量：強反向支路 $g<-1$ 使 $[-h(t)]_+>2\nu\\|\Delta S(t)\\|_2^2$（第9節），並為算子分解 $\widehat Q=-ge_D+Q_\perp$ 的對齊分量（第11節）。 | The scalar used in Sections 9 and 11: the strong-opposing branch $g<-1$ forces $[-h(t)]_+>2\nu\\|\Delta S(t)\\|_2^2$ (Section 9), and $g$ is the aligned component of the decomposition $\widehat Q=-ge_D+Q_\perp$ (Section 11). | C4-J |
| `Q_\perp,\quad \\|Q_\perp\\|_2^2=r_\nu^2-g^2` | 第11節沿用C4-I分解 $\widehat Q=-ge_D+Q_\perp$ 中的正交分量，當 $r_\nu\gg1$ 且 $\|g\|\le1$ 時 $\\|Q_\perp\\|_2\sim r_\nu$。 | The orthogonal component in the C4-I decomposition $\widehat Q=-ge_D+Q_\perp$ recalled in Section 11; when $r_\nu\gg1$ and $\|g\|\le1$, $\\|Q_\perp\\|_2\sim r_\nu$. | C4-J |
| `\textbf{Operator Orthogonal Congestion}` | 第11節命名的補償動機：大算子範數由 $Q_\perp$（渦度平方或正交平流/應變平方項）承擔而不直接對 $E_1'$ 收費，目前無finite norm budget禁止其反覆出現。 | The compensation motif named in Section 11: large operator norm is carried by $Q_\perp$ (vorticity-quadratic or orthogonal advection/strain-square terms) without directly charging $E_1'$, and no finite norm budget currently forbids its recurrence. | C4-J |
| `M_\chi'=-B_\chi-P_\chi` | 第13節給出的恆等式，$B_\chi$ 為局部二次平均迫力、$P_\chi$ 為局部壓力Hessian平均迫力，兩者共同決定伴隨核心均值 $M_\chi$ 的變化率。 | The identity from Section 13, where $B_\chi$ is the local quadratic mean forcing and $P_\chi$ the local pressure-Hessian mean forcing, jointly determining the rate of change of the adjoint-core mean $M_\chi$. | C4-J |
| `\mathfrak B_I,\ \mathfrak V_M(I),\ \mathfrak P_I` | 第14節在時間區間 $I$ 與核心尺度 $R$ 上定義的三個標準化量，滿足逐點不等式 $\|B_\chi\|\le\|M_\chi'\|+\|P_\chi\|$ 導出的 $\mathfrak B_I\le\mathfrak V_M(I)+\mathfrak P_I$。 | The three normalized quantities defined in Section 14 over interval $I$ and core scale $R$, satisfying $\mathfrak B_I\le\mathfrak V_M(I)+\mathfrak P_I$ derived from the pointwise bound $\|B_\chi\|\le\|M_\chi'\|+\|P_\chi\|$. | C4-J |
| `\mathfrak V_M(I)\ge(1-\varepsilon)b_0` | 第15節定理：若 $\mathfrak B_I\ge b_0>0$ 且壓力衝量受壓 $\mathfrak P_I\le\varepsilon b_0$，則 $\mathfrak V_M(I)\ge(1-\varepsilon)b_0$，即壓力若不re-entry，平均應變變化必支付固定debt。 | The theorem of Section 15: if $\mathfrak B_I\ge b_0>0$ and the pressure impulse is suppressed to $\mathfrak P_I\le\varepsilon b_0$, then $\mathfrak V_M(I)\ge(1-\varepsilon)b_0$ — if pressure does not re-enter, mean-strain variation must pay a fixed debt. | C4-J |
| `Q(x,t)=S^2+\frac14\omega\otimes\omega-\frac14\|\omega\|^2I` | 第17節定義的張量場，是後續barycenter coherence與seven-point cancellation分析的基本對象。 | The tensor field defined in Section 17, the basic object underlying the subsequent barycenter-coherence and seven-point cancellation analysis. | C4-J |
| `A=\int\chi\|Q\|dx,\ B=\int\chi Qdx` | 第17節在固定 $(t,\chi)$ 下定義的純量 $A$ 與矩陣值積分 $B$，用以構成coherence $\kappa=\|B\|/A$。 | The scalar $A$ and matrix-valued integral $B$ defined in Section 17 at fixed $(t,\chi)$, used to form the coherence $\kappa=\|B\|/A$. | C4-J |
| `\kappa=\frac{\|B\|}{A}` | 第17-18節定義的量，等於局部二次方向 $U(x)$ 在機率測度 $\mu$ 下之重心範數，是本輪判定quadratic cancellation的中心尺度。 | The quantity defined in Sections 17-18, equal to the barycenter norm of the local quadratic direction $U(x)$ under the probability measure $\mu$, the central scalar governing quadratic cancellation in this round. | C4-J |
| `U(x)=\frac{Q(x)}{\|Q(x)\|}\in\operatorname{Sym}(3)` | 第18節在 $Q(x)\ne0$ 之區域定義的正規化對稱矩陣，取值於 $\operatorname{Sym}(3)\simeq\mathbb R^6$，是seven-point witness定理中被表示的對象。 | The normalized symmetric matrix defined in Section 18 on the region where $Q(x)\ne0$, valued in $\operatorname{Sym}(3)\simeq\mathbb R^6$, the object represented by the seven-point witness theorem. | C4-J |
| `\sum_{i=1}^{m}\alpha_iU_i=\frac{B}{A},\ m\le7` | 第20節定理20.1，藉 $\operatorname{Sym}(3)\simeq\mathbb R^6$ 上的Carathéodory定理證明若 $A>0$，則至多7個正規化局部二次矩陣之凸組合即可表示重心 $B/A$，其範數等於 $\kappa$。 | Theorem 20.1 of Section 20, using Carathéodory's theorem on $\operatorname{Sym}(3)\simeq\mathbb R^6$ to show that if $A>0$, a convex combination of at most 7 normalized local quadratic matrices already represents the barycenter $B/A$, with norm equal to $\kappa$. | C4-J |
| `\mathfrak V_M(I_n)\ge v_0>0\ \vee\ \kappa_n^{quad}\le\kappa_0<1` | 第25節定理25.1，證明對收縮伴隨核心事件列，若局部二次迫力非退化且壓力振盪不進入固定下界支路，則沿無窮子列必recurrent出現Mean-Variation Motif（J-P1）或Quadratic Orientation-Cancellation Motif（J-P2）。 | Theorem 25.1 of Section 25: for a sequence of shrinking adjoint-core events with nondegenerate local quadratic forcing and pressure oscillation avoiding a fixed lower-bound branch, an infinite subsequence must recurrently exhibit either the Mean-Variation Motif (J-P1) or the Quadratic Orientation-Cancellation Motif (J-P2). | C4-J |
| `\Pi_{R}^{(2)}` | 第27節引入的量：當mean variation與quadratic cancellation兩種補償皆失敗時，$\Pi_{R_n}^{(2)}\ge\pi_0>0$ 迫使壓力進入critical concentration branch。 | The quantity introduced in Section 27: when both mean-variation and quadratic-cancellation compensation fail, $\Pi_{R_n}^{(2)}\ge\pi_0>0$ forces pressure into the critical concentration branch. | C4-J |
| `\mathcal C=\{T,O,M,Q,P,D\}` | 第28節命名、第40節形式化為集合 $\mathcal C$ 的六種殘餘動機——T時序脈衝、O算子夾角、M平均變化、Q二次方向消去、P壓力集中、D導數閘門缺陷——是C4封階論斷的核心分類。 | The six residual motifs named in Section 28 and formalized as the set $\mathcal C$ in Section 40 — T (temporal pulse), O (operator-angle), M (mean-variation), Q (quadratic orientation-cancellation), P (pressure concentration), D (derivative-gate defect) — the central classification underlying the C4 closure claim. | C4-J |
| `\text{C4 branch/synchronization phase is structurally closed}` | 第40節研究方案層級的定理，斷言所有recurrent UV singular events都可路由到synchronized結構或有限補償動機族 $\mathcal C$，故C4封階，但此僅為research-program phase closure，非Navier–Stokes正則性定理。 | The research-program-level theorem of Section 40, asserting every recurrent UV singular event routes to synchronized structures or the finite motif family $\mathcal C$, so the C4 phase closes — but only as a research-program phase closure, not a Navier-Stokes regularity theorem. | C4-J |
| `\Theta_j^{C5}=\left\langle\mu_j^{mid},\mu_j^{op,+},\mu_j^{op,-},\mu_j^{press},\mu_j^{work},\mathcal U_j^{(7)},\mathsf D_j\right\rangle` | 第44節為下一階段C5-A提出的狀態向量，將每個record window的中間應變、算子正/負成長、壓力、work-variation測度與seven-point witness、導數閘門缺陷打包為單一metadata物件。 | The state vector proposed in Section 44 for the forthcoming C5-A paper, packaging each record window's middle-strain, operator growth/opposing, pressure, and work-variation measures together with the seven-point witness and derivative-gate defect into one metadata object. | C4-J |
| `\{UV,\ Strain,\ \lambda_2^+,\ Operator,\ Helicity,\ Pressure,\ Derivative\}` | 第30-37節逐一評定UV、Strain、Middle $\lambda_2^+$、Operator、Helicity、Pressure、Derivative geometry之最終同步狀態，並在第37節彙整成表，是本輪三大任務之一。 | Sections 30-37 assess the final synchronization status of UV, Strain, Middle $\lambda_2^+$, Operator, Helicity, Pressure, and Derivative geometry individually, summarized in the Section 37 table — one of the round's three main tasks. | C4-J |

## C5-A

| 符號 Symbol | 中文定義 | Definition (EN) | 出處 Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `s` | 在第2節中定義，將實體收縮時間窗線性映射至(0,1)區間的無因次化變數。 | Defined in Section 2 as the dimensionless variable linearly mapping the physical shrinking time window to the (0,1) interval. | C5-A |
| `\widehat\Theta_j^{time}` | 在第2節中引入，記錄單位時間重整化後保留的相對黏滯尺度，並映射至[0,1]區間。 | Introduced in Section 2, mapping the preserved relative viscous scale under unit-time renormalization to the [0,1] interval. | C5-A |
| `\mathcal K_{\rm anc}` | 在第3節中定義，包含尺度比例與位移距離之緊緻化座標以及方向向量的空間。 | Defined in Section 3 as the space containing the compactified coordinates of scale ratios, displacement distances, and direction vectors. | C5-A |
| `\mu_j^{mid}` | 在第4節中定義，以中度應變特徵值與應變張量範數平方之積分作為權重的單位時間機率測度。 | Defined in Section 4 as a unit-time probability measure weighted by the integral of the middle-strain eigenvalue and squared strain tensor norm. | C5-A |
| `\alpha_j^{mid}` | 在第4節中定義，為能量下降量與中度應變總增量之比值。 | Defined in Section 4 as the ratio of energy drop to the total middle-strain growth on the window. | C5-A |
| `\delta_j^{mid}` | 在第4節中定義，為應變耗散積分與中度應變總增量之比值。 | Defined in Section 4 as the ratio of the strain dissipation integral to the total middle-strain growth on the window. | C5-A |
| `h_j(t)` | 在第5節中定義，為包含黏滯耗散與渦度交互作用的算子動能一階導數。 | Defined in Section 5 as the first derivative of the operator kinetic energy including viscous dissipation and vorticity interaction. | C5-A |
| `\mu_j^{op,+}` | 在第5節中定義，以算子能量成長率正部為權重歸一化的次機率測度。 | Defined in Section 5 as the normalized subprobability measure weighted by the positive part of the operator energy growth rate. | C5-A |
| `\mu_j^{op,-}` | 在第5節中定義，以算子能量衰減量為權重歸一化的次機率測度。 | Defined in Section 5 as the normalized subprobability measure weighted by the negative part of the operator energy growth rate. | C5-A |
| `\beta_j^{op}` | 在第5節中定義，反映正負算子能量成長差異相對於總變差的比例。 | Defined in Section 5 as the ratio of the net operator energy change to the total variation. | C5-A |
| `\rho` | 在第6節中定義，為容許非線性交互項與耗散項之比值趨於無限大而引入的[0,1]區間座標。 | Defined in Section 6 as a [0,1] coordinate to accommodate the ratio of nonlinear interactions to dissipation blowing up to infinity. | C5-A |
| `\gamma` | 在第6節中定義，將算子幾何的對齊參數映射至[-1,1]區間的緊緻座標。 | Defined in Section 6 as a compact coordinate mapping the alignment parameter of the operator geometry into the [-1,1] interval. | C5-A |
| `\mathcal K_{\rm op}` | 在第6節中定義，包含所有緊緻化算子比值與角度座標的封閉緊緻狀態空間。 | Defined in Section 6 as the closed, compact state space containing all compactified operator ratio and angle coordinates. | C5-A |
| `\eta_j^{op}` | 在第7節中定義，以總變差為權重分布於單位時間與算子角度緊緻空間上的機率測度。 | Defined in Section 7 as a probability measure on the unit time and operator-angle compact space, weighted by absolute operator growth. | C5-A |
| `a_{M,j}` | 在第8節中定義，為伴隨核心無因次化變差總量映射至[0,1]區間的緊緻振幅。 | Defined in Section 8 as the compactified amplitude mapping the dimensionless total variation of the adjoint core to the [0,1] interval. | C5-A |
| `\mathbf m_j^M` | 在第8節中定義，以伴隨核心的時間導數分布所建構的向量測度。 | Defined in Section 8 as a vector measure constructed from the time derivative of the adjoint core. | C5-A |
| `\kappa_j^Q` | 在第9節中定義，衡量二次空間分布淨量相對於總強度的比值，趨於零代表強烈抵消。 | Defined in Section 9 as the ratio of net quadratic spatial distribution to absolute intensity, where approaching zero indicates strong cancellation. | C5-A |
| `\mathcal K_Q` | 在第9節中定義，透過卡拉西奧多里約化記錄二次抵消特徵方向與權重的有限維緊緻空間。 | Defined in Section 9 as a finite-dimensional compact space recording the discrete directions and weights of quadratic cancellation via Carathéodory reduction. | C5-A |
| `\nu_j^P` | 在第10節中定義，在特定壓力激發時刻將局部壓力場分佈正規化所得的空間測度。 | Defined in Section 10 as the normalized spatial measure of the local pressure field at a specific active pressure time. | C5-A |
| `a_j^P` | 在第10節中定義，將無因次局部壓力總量映射至[0,1]區間的狀態變數。 | Defined in Section 10 as the state variable mapping the dimensionless total local pressure mass to the [0,1] interval. | C5-A |
| `\widehat\Pi_j^{(2)}` | 在第10節中定義，捕捉對海森矩陣敏感的局部壓力場震盪並映射至[0,1]區間。 | Defined in Section 10 as the parameter capturing the Hessian-sensitive local pressure oscillation, mapped to the [0,1] interval. | C5-A |
| `\widehat{\mathfrak L}_j` | 在第11節中定義，將高階導數閉包負載映射至[0,1]區間的狀態變數。 | Defined in Section 11 as the state variable mapping the best higher-order derivative closure load to the [0,1] interval. | C5-A |
| `a_j^{motif}` | 在第12節中定義，記錄六種補償圖案活躍狀態的布林向量，用於抽取最終穩定的圖案組合。 | Defined in Section 12 as a boolean vector recording the active states of the six compensation motifs, used to extract an eventually constant pattern. | C5-A |
| `\Theta_j^{C5}` | 在第13節中定義，統合所有緊緻化測度、振幅與幾何元資料的補償圖案狀態向量。 | Defined in Section 13 as the state vector unifying all compactified measures, amplitudes, and geometric metadata for the compensation motifs. | C5-A |
| `\Theta_\ast^{C5}` | 在第14節與第25節中證明存在，為無窮生存梯隊在緊緻化拓樸下收斂的元資料相容性極限。 | Proved to exist in Sections 14 and 25, representing the convergent metadata compatibility limit of an infinite survivor ladder under compact topologies. | C5-A |
| `\mathfrak O_{j,n}` | 在第19節中定義，用於量化不同時間尺度下中度應變與算子正成長測度的重疊程度。 | Defined in Section 19 as the integral quantifying the overlap between the middle-strain and operator positive growth measures at varying time scales. | C5-A |

## C5-B

| 符號 Symbol | 中文定義 | Definition (EN) | 出處 Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `f_j^M(s)` | 第3節將紀錄窗內中間應變荷載密度 m_j 對總 toll \mathcal{M}_j 正規化，得到 [0,1] 上積分為一的非負密度 f_j^M。 | Section 3 normalizes the middle-strain load density m_j against the total middle toll \mathcal{M}_j to a nonnegative unit-mass density f_j^M on [0,1]. | C5-B |
| `f_j^+(s)` | 第4節將算子增長正部 [h_j]_+ 對總正變分 P_j 正規化，得到單位質量密度 f_j^+。 | Section 4 normalizes the positive part [h_j]_+ of operator growth against the total positive variation P_j, yielding the unit-mass density f_j^+. | C5-B |
| `f_j^-(s)` | 第5節在 N_j>0 時將 [-h_j]_+ 對總反相變分正規化為單位質量密度 f_j^-，否則令其恆為零。 | Section 5 sets f_j^- to the unit-mass normalization of [-h_j]_+ when N_j>0, and to the zero function when N_j=0. | C5-B |
| `f_j^+ f_j^-=0` | 第6節由點態 [h_j]_+[-h_j]_+=0 推出兩正規化算子密度幾乎處處相乘為零，作為本輪最基本的精確時相約束。 | Section 6 records the exact temporal phase constraint that the two operator-sign normalized densities multiply to zero a.e., inherited from the pointwise identity [h_j]_+[-h_j]_+=0. | C5-B |
| `\vartheta=(a,b,c)\in\mathbb{Q}_{>0}^3` | 第7節固定正有理閾值 ϑ=(a,b,c)，分別切割中間、正算子與反相算子正規化荷載的活躍集合。 | Section 7 fixes a rational triple ϑ=(a,b,c) of positive thresholds that cut the three normalized loads into active versus inactive phases. | C5-B |
| `\chi_{j,M}^\vartheta,\ \chi_{j,+}^\vartheta,\ \chi_{j,-}^\vartheta` | 第7節分別以 {f_j^M≥a}、{f_j^+≥b}、{f_j^-≥c} 的特徵函數定義三個二值時相指示。 | Section 7 defines the three binary phase indicators as the characteristic functions of the threshold-active sets {f_j^M≥a}, {f_j^+≥b} and {f_j^-≥c}. | C5-B |
| `X_j^\vartheta(s)` | 第7節把三個閾值指示函數打包成取值於 {0,1}^3 的聯合相位向量，作為著色時間微狀態。 | Section 7 packages the three threshold indicators into the joint phase vector X_j^ϑ taking values in {0,1}^3, which is the colored temporal microstate. | C5-B |
| `\mathcal{A}` | 第8節由 χ_+χ_-=0 把允許相位狀態限制為六個離散字元 {000,100,010,001,110,101}，並禁止 011 與 111。 | Section 8 restricts admissible phase states, via χ_+χ_-=0, to the six-element discrete alphabet {000,100,010,001,110,101}, forbidding 011 and 111. | C5-B |
| `Y_j^\vartheta` | 第9節把正規化時間 s 與相位向量 X_j^ϑ(s) 對 Lebesgue 測度 ds 的 push-forward 定義為取值於 P([0,1]×A) 且第一邊緣固定為 ds 的著色時間圖測度。 | Section 9 defines the colored temporal graph measure as the push-forward of Lebesgue measure ds under s↦(s,X_j^ϑ(s)), landing in P([0,1]×A) with first marginal ds. | C5-B |
| `Y_\ast^\vartheta=ds\,\nu_s^\vartheta` | 第10節定理10.1（C5-B.1）抽出弱極限 Y_*^ϑ，其第一邊緣仍為 ds，並纖維化為幾乎處處取值於 P(A) 的未解析時相分佈 ν_s^ϑ。 | Section 10 (Theorem 10.1 / C5-B.1) extracts a weak limit Y_*^ϑ with first marginal ds, disintegrating as ds ν_s^ϑ with ν_s^ϑ∈P(A) for a.e. s. | C5-B |
| `\mathfrak{Y}_\ast` | 第11節對可數集 Q_{>0}^3 對角抽取，得到同一子列上對所有有理 ϑ 同時成立的 Young 極限族，並稱之為時間相位譜。 | Section 11 diagonalizes over the countable set Q_{>0}^3 to obtain a single subsequence along which Y_j^ϑ⇀Y_*^ϑ for every rational ϑ, called the temporal phase spectrum. | C5-B |
| `C_{\ast,M+}^\vartheta` | 第14節以 Young 測度在相位 110 上的質量定義中間／正算子共活佔空比，有限尺度值為 C_{j,M+}^ϑ、極限值為 C_{*,M+}^ϑ。 | Section 14 defines the coactive duty as the Y-mass of the phase 110, with finite-scale value C_{j,M+}^ϑ and Young-limit value C_{*,M+}^ϑ. | C5-B |
| `\bar\chi_M^\vartheta(s),\ \bar\chi_+^\vartheta(s),\ \bar\chi_-^\vartheta(s)` | 第18節將纖維測度 ν_s^ϑ 對各相位座標積分，得到正規化時間 s 處的局部時相佔比重心投影。 | Section 18 integrates the disintegrated Young fiber ν_s^ϑ against each phase coordinate to obtain the barycentric (mean) phase fractions at normalized time s. | C5-B |
| `c_{M+}^\vartheta(s)` | 第18節以纖維測度對乘積 ξ_M ξ_+ 的積分定義微觀共活密度，量測同一微狀態上中間與正算子同時活躍的局部質量。 | Section 18 defines the microscopic coactivation density as the fiber integral of the product ξ_M ξ_+, measuring local mass of simultaneous middle and positive-operator activity. | C5-B |
| `\operatorname{Cov}_{M+}^\vartheta(s)` | 第19節以微觀共活減去重心分數乘積定義 Cov_{M+}，在完全互斥且兩分數皆正時嚴格為負，用以量化反相關時相混合。 | Section 19 defines the temporal phase covariance as microscopic coactivation minus the product of barycentric fractions, which is strictly negative under complete exclusion with both fractions positive. | C5-B |
| `\mathcal{K}_{\mathrm{op}}^\dagger=\mathcal{K}_{\mathrm{op}}\cup\{\partial\}` | 第20節在 C5-A 的算子緊集 K_op 上加入墓地點 ∂，使兩算子閾值皆不活躍的時刻可標記為 ∂ 而空間仍緊。 | Section 20 adjoins a cemetery point ∂ to the C5-A operator compact K_op so that times inactive for both operator thresholds can be marked while remaining in a compact space. | C5-B |
| `\kappa_j^{op}(s)` | 第20節在 χ_{j,+}+χ_{j,-}>0 的正規化時刻把 κ_j^{op} 設為 Φ_op(r_ν,ζ)，否則設為墓地點 ∂。 | Section 20 attaches κ_j^{op}(s)=Φ_op(r_ν,ζ) on normalized times where at least one operator threshold is active, and sets κ_j^{op}=∂ otherwise. | C5-B |
| `\widetilde{Y}_j^\vartheta` | 第21節把 (s,X_j^ϑ(s),κ_j^{op}(s)) 對 ds 的 push-forward 放到緊空間 [0,1]×A×K_op^† 上，從而可抽取標記 Young 弱極限。 | Section 21 push-forwards (s,X_j^ϑ(s),κ_j^{op}(s)) under ds onto the compact [0,1]×A×K_op^†, producing a marked Young measure with extractable weak limits. | C5-B |
| `\gamma\ge\tfrac12\ \text{on}\ \mathrm{supp}(\xi_+=1);\quad\gamma\le\tfrac12\ \text{on}\ \mathrm{supp}(\xi_-=1)` | 第23節定理 C5-B.4 要求標記 Young 極限在正算子相位支撐閉包上滿足 γ≥1/2、在反相相位上滿足 γ≤1/2，並保持 ξ_+ξ_-=0。 | Section 23 (C5-B.4) forces the marked Young limit to satisfy γ≥1/2 on the support closure of ξ_+=1, γ≤1/2 on that of ξ_-=1, together with ξ_+ξ_-=0. | C5-B |
| `\mathfrak{c}_f^\infty` | 第25節先取尾質量 limsup_j ∫_{f_j>K} f_j，再令 K→∞ 得到 [0,1] 中的漸近集中質量，用以捕捉 vanishing-duty 高振幅尖峰。 | Section 25 defines the tail mass 𝔠_f(K) as the limsup of load above height K, then takes K→∞ to obtain the asymptotic concentration mass 𝔠_f^∞∈[0,1] that captures vanishing-duty high-amplitude spikes. | C5-B |
| `\mathfrak{c}_M,\ \mathfrak{c}_+,\ \mathfrak{c}_-` | 第27節分別對 f^M、f^+ 與（在反相活躍反覆出現時）f^- 取漸近集中質量，作為荷載加權的時間集中缺陷座標。 | Section 27 evaluates the asymptotic concentration mass on f^M, f^+ and (when opposing activity is recurrent) f^-, yielding the load-weighted temporal concentration coordinates. | C5-B |
| `\mathfrak{c}_f^\infty=1` | 第30節定理30.1（C5-B.6）證明：若對每個 a>0 皆有 \|{f_j≥a}\|→0，則漸近集中質量必等於 1，即全部荷載質量轉成集中缺陷。 | Section 30 (Theorem 30.1 / C5-B.6) proves that if \|{f_j≥a}\|→0 for every a>0, then 𝔠_f^∞=1, so the entire unit load mass becomes a concentration defect. | C5-B |
| `\mathrm{B\text{-}COACT}\vee\mathrm{B\text{-}OSC}\vee\mathrm{B\text{-}CONC}` | 第33節定理33.1（C5-B.7）斷言中間與正算子正規化荷載序列在抽出的 C5-B 子列上必落入真共活、主體 Young 相位分離／振盪、或時間荷載集中三者之一。 | Section 33 (Theorem 33.1 / C5-B.7) asserts that along the extracted C5-B subsequence the middle and positive-operator normalized-load sequences fall into genuine coactivation, bulk Young-phase segregation/oscillation, or temporal load concentration. | C5-B |
| `\Lambda_j` | 第36節取中間與正算子荷載測度的平均作為 [0,1] 上的共同機率測度 Λ_j，用以聯合支配兩種顏色。 | Section 36 averages the middle and positive-operator load measures into a common probability Λ_j on [0,1] that jointly dominates both colors. | C5-B |
| `z_{j,M},\ z_{j,+}` | 第36節以 Λ_j 為底取 μ_j^M、μ_j^+ 的 Radon–Nikodym 導數之半，得到 Λ_j-a.e. 和為一的顏色分數。 | Section 36 takes half the Radon–Nikodym derivatives of μ_j^M and μ_j^+ with respect to Λ_j, yielding color fractions that sum to 1 Λ_j-a.e. | C5-B |
| `\Upsilon_j` | 第37節把 (s,z_{j,M},z_{j,+}) 對 Λ_j 的 push-forward 放到 [0,1]×Δ_2 上；第38節證明其弱極限 Υ_* 的重心投影恰好恢復一階分色荷載測度。 | Section 37 push-forwards (s,z_{j,M},z_{j,+}) under Λ_j onto [0,1]×Δ_2; Section 38 shows that the weak limit Υ_* recovers the separate first-order load measures as barycentric projections. | C5-B |
| `C_j^{a\to b}(\ell),\ C_\ast^{a\to b}(\ell)` | 第42節對二值閾值相位以固定延遲 ℓ 積分乘積，並對有理 ℓ 對角抽取極限 C_*^{a→b}(ℓ) 作為粗轉換譜。 | Section 42 integrates products of binary threshold phases at a fixed lag ℓ and extracts limits C_*^{a→b}(ℓ) over rational lags as a coarse transition spectrum. | C5-B |
| `\beta_j^{op}` | 第44節以 (P_j-N_j)/(P_j+N_j)>0 記錄正增長對總變分的偏置；極限 β_*>0 表示正增長質量佔優，β_*=0 則為算子補償循環邊界態。 | Section 44 records the strictly positive bias (P_j-N_j)/(P_j+N_j) of operator growth; β_*→positive means positive-growth mass dominance, while β_*=0 is an operator compensation-cycle boundary state. | C5-B |
| `\Theta_\ast^{C5B}` | 第45節把 C5-A 極限、時間相位譜、標記 Young 譜、三個集中質量、荷載著色 Young 圖與固定延遲相關元資料打包為增強相容態 Θ_*^{C5B}。 | Section 45 packages the C5-A limit together with the phase Young spectrum, marked Young spectrum, concentration masses, load-colored Young graph and lag-correlation metadata into the enhanced compatibility state Θ_*^{C5B}. | C5-B |
| `\mathrm{T1},\mathrm{T2},\mathrm{T3},\mathrm{T4}` | 第46節（C5-B.8）將紀錄窗正規化下的中間／算子補償分類為真共活（T1）、主體 Young 相位分離（T2）、荷載集中（T3）與未解析的次 Young 排序／相關缺陷（T4）。 | Section 46 (C5-B.8) classifies middle/operator temporal compensation at the first microstructure level into genuine coactivation (T1), bulk Young-phase segregation (T2), load concentration (T3), and unresolved sub-Young ordering/correlation defect (T4). | C5-B |

## C5-D

| 符號 Symbol | 中文定義 | Definition (EN) | 出處 Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Q(S,\omega)` | 在第2節中定義為應變平方與渦度張量二次項的組合，用以捕捉N-S方程式局部真實的非線性交互作用。 | Defined in Section 2 as the combination of strain squared and vorticity tensor quadratic terms to capture local true nonlinear interactions of the N-S equations. | C5-D |
| `K` | 在第3節中定義為跡數為零、弗羅貝尼烏斯範數為1且中間特徵值嚴格大於零的單位應變張量方向。 | Defined in Section 3 as a trace-free, unit Frobenius norm strain tensor direction with a strictly positive middle eigenvalue. | C5-D |
| `k_1, k_2, k_3` | 在第3節中引入，表示單位張量 $K$ 的三個由小到大排序的特徵值，其中 $k_1 < 0$ 且 $k_2, k_3 > 0$。 | Introduced in Section 3 as the three eigenvalues of the unit tensor $K$, ordered from smallest to largest, where $k_1 < 0$ and $k_2, k_3 > 0$. | C5-D |
| `e_1` | 在第3節中定義為對應於最小特徵值 $k_1$ 的單位特徵向量，代表局部流體形變中最強的壓縮方向。 | Defined in Section 3 as the unit eigenvector corresponding to the smallest eigenvalue $k_1$, representing the direction of strongest compression in local fluid deformation. | C5-D |
| `\theta_K` | 在第4節中定義為 $k_2$ 與 $k_3$ 的乘積，用以量化正規化應變形狀遠離退化邊界 $\lambda_2=0$ 的程度。 | Defined in Section 4 as the product of $k_2$ and $k_3$, quantifying how far the normalized strain shape is from the degenerate boundary $\lambda_2=0$. | C5-D |
| `H_K` | 在第5節中定義的半空間測試泛函，能確保任意渦度與應變平方項在其作用下皆嚴格為正。 | Defined in Section 5 as a half-space test functional under which both arbitrary vorticity and strain-square contributions are strictly positive. | C5-D |
| `\delta_K` | 在第9節中定義的容許擾動半徑，確保在該半徑內的應變方向仍保持二次半空間正定性。 | Defined in Section 9 as the allowable perturbation radius ensuring that strain directions within it maintain quadratic half-space positivity. | C5-D |
| `\mathcal{C}_K` | 在第9節中定義為以 $K$ 為中心且半徑為 $\delta_K$ 的單位應變方向集合，保證局部二次張量的一致同調性。 | Defined in Section 9 as the set of unit strain directions within radius $\delta_K$ from $K$, guaranteeing uniform coherence of the local quadratic tensor. | C5-D |
| `\widehat{H}_K` | 在第12節中定義為正規化後的 $H_K$，用以測量二次張量落入嚴格半空間的下界餘裕。 | Defined in Section 12 as the normalized $H_K$, used to measure the margin by which the quadratic tensor falls into a strict half-space. | C5-D |
| `\gamma_K` | 在第12節中定義為嚴格正的下界常數，代表強中間錐內二次方向的幾何一致性強度。 | Defined in Section 12 as the strictly positive lower bound constant representing the geometric coherence strength of quadratic directions within the strong-middle cone. | C5-D |
| `\mathcal{H}_K^+` | 在第13節中定義的五維球面子集，包含所有由強中間應變錐產生的正規化二次張量方向，其凸包不包含原點。 | Defined in Section 13 as the subset of the five-dimensional sphere containing all normalized quadratic tensor directions generated by the strong-middle strain cone, whose convex hull strictly excludes the origin. | C5-D |
| `A_\chi^Q` | 在第14節中定義為局部二次張量範數的空間積分，代表該區域內的總二次交互作用強度。 | Defined in Section 14 as the spatial integral of the local quadratic tensor norm, representing the total quadratic interaction intensity in the region. | C5-D |
| `B_\chi^Q` | 在第14節中定義為局部二次張量本身的空間加權積分矩陣。 | Defined in Section 14 as the spatially weighted integral matrix of the local quadratic tensor itself. | C5-D |
| `\kappa_\chi^Q` | 在第14節中定義為均值矩陣大小與總二次質量之比，用以量化七點抵消機制的失效程度。 | Defined in Section 14 as the ratio of the mean matrix magnitude to the total quadratic mass, quantifying the failure of the Seven-Point cancellation mechanism. | C5-D |
| `\varepsilon_\chi^K` | 在第18節中定義為落在強中間錐外部的二次質量比例，是七點抵消存活的必要代價。 | Defined in Section 18 as the fraction of quadratic mass falling outside the strong-middle cone, acting as a necessary cost for Seven-Point cancellation survival. | C5-D |
| `\bar{S}_R` | 在第24節中定義為尺度 $R$ 上的空間加權平均應變，為平均應變錐分析的基礎對象。 | Defined in Section 24 as the spatially weighted mean strain at scale $R$, serving as the base object for mean-strain cone analysis. | C5-D |
| `K_R` | 在第24節中定義為 $\bar{S}_R$ 除以其範數所得到的平均應變單位方向矩陣。 | Defined in Section 24 as the unit mean strain direction matrix obtained by dividing $\bar{S}_R$ by its norm. | C5-D |
| `\eta_R^S` | 在第25節中定義為局部應變相對於平均應變矩陣大小的最大偏差比例。 | Defined in Section 25 as the ratio of maximum local strain deviation to the magnitude of the mean strain matrix. | C5-D |
| `\mu_R` | 在第31節中定義的無因次量，代表平均應變在給定尺度下的強度，若夠大則自動保證非退化的二次強度。 | Defined in Section 31 as a dimensionless quantity representing the mean strain intensity at a given scale, ensuring nondegenerate quadratic intensity when sufficiently large. | C5-D |
| `P_\chi` | 在第32節中定義為伴隨壓力張量的積分，當二次抵消失效時被迫重新進入平衡方程並承擔定向補償。 | Defined in Section 32 as the integral of the adjoint pressure tensor, which is forced to re-enter the balance equations and assume oriented compensation when quadratic cancellation fails. | C5-D |
| `\Pi_R^{(2)}` | 在第34節中引入，用以量化壓力相對於仿射函數的局部偏差，受到半空間幾何的強制下界約束。 | Introduced in Section 34 to quantify the local deviation of pressure from affine functions, forced into a lower bound by the half-space geometry. | C5-D |
| `F` | 在第36節中定義的無跡對稱矩陣，代表從遠方主導局部核心的共同背景壓力海森。 | Defined in Section 36 as a trace-free symmetric matrix representing the common background pressure Hessian dominating local cores from afar. | C5-D |
| `G(e)` | 在第38節中定義為從半空間泛函中提取出與遠場壓力配對的無跡部分，是引發多核心凸包阻礙的關鍵物件。 | Defined in Section 38 as the trace-free part extracted from the half-space functional that pairs with far pressure, serving as the key object triggering multi-core convex-hull obstructions. | C5-D |
| `\nu_j^S` | 在第45節中定義的局部極限測度，用於捕捉應變方向在五維球面上的機率分佈與集中現象。 | Defined in Section 45 as a local limit measure used to capture the probability distribution and concentration of strain directions on the five-dimensional sphere. | C5-D |
| `\nu_j^Q` | 在第45節中定義的測度，追蹤由七點抵消機制等極限程序產生的正規化二次張量分佈。 | Defined in Section 45 as a measure tracking the limit distribution of normalized quadratic tensors arising from procedures like the Seven-Point cancellation mechanism. | C5-D |
| `\vartheta(S)` | 在第51節中定義為未正規化應變矩陣的中間正特徵值與最大特徵值之積除以範數平方，提供半空間機制的逐點餘裕來源。 | Defined in Section 51 as the product of the positive middle and largest eigenvalues divided by the squared norm of the unnormalized strain, providing the pointwise margin source for the half-space mechanism. | C5-D |
| `\Theta_\ast^{SM}` | 在第58節中定義的真實ETN更新，囊括了極限狀態下的應變、二次測度、強中間餘裕以及壓縮軸投影組態等空間幾何元數據。 | Defined in Section 58 as the True ETN update encapsulating spatial geometric metadata such as strain/quadratic measures, strong-middle margin, and compressive-axis configurations in the limit state. | C5-D |

## C5-E

| 符號 Symbol | 中文定義 | Definition (EN) | 出處 Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `\vartheta(S)` | 第 2 節對非零無跡對稱應變引入正規化中間隙形狀變數，並在中間特徵值非正時令其為零。 | Section 2 introduces the normalized middle-gap shape variable of a nonzero trace-free symmetric strain, set to zero when the middle eigenvalue is nonpositive. | C5-E |
| `\xi_2(S)` | 第 2 節將 Miller 中間特徵值正部除以 Frobenius 範數，作為正中間扇區的尺度不變形狀座標。 | Section 2 normalizes Miller's positive middle eigenvalue by the Frobenius norm, yielding the scale-invariant shape coordinate of the positive-middle sector. | C5-E |
| `\lambda_2^+(S)` | 第 1.1 與第 2 節沿用 Miller 的 λ₂⁺(S)=max(λ₂(S),0)，作為應變中間通道的尺度臨界正則幾何。 | Sections 1.1 and 2 take Miller's λ₂⁺(S)=max(λ₂(S),0) as the scale-critical regularity geometry of the middle-strain channel. | C5-E |
| `Q` | 第 0 節將 C5-D 的七點零重心二次抵消 motif 視為本輪必須轉成場缺陷的殘餘補償器。 | Section 0 treats the C5-D seven-point zero-barycenter quadratic cancellation motif as the residual compensator that this round must convert into field defects. | C5-E |
| `\|Q(S,\omega)\|\ge c_Q\delta(\|S\|^2+\|\omega\|^2)` | 第 4 節 C5-E.2 斷言：在 ϑ≥δ>0 的區域，萬有常數 c_Q>0 使 \|Q\| 與 \|S\|²+\|ω\|² 可比較，故 Q 加權集中不是矩陣正規化假象。 | Section 4 (C5-E.2) asserts that on {ϑ≥δ>0} a universal c_Q>0 makes \|Q\| comparable to \|S\|²+\|ω\|², so Q-weighted concentration is genuine quadratic-activity concentration. | C5-E |
| `A_j^Q` | 第 6 節以選定伴隨／局部核截斷 χ_j≥0 定義活躍 Q motif 的總質量 A_j^Q=∫ χ_j \|Q_j\| dx。 | Section 6 defines the total mass A_j^Q=∫ χ_j \|Q_j\| dx of an active Q motif against a selected adjoint/local-core cutoff χ_j≥0. | C5-E |
| `\nu_j^Q` | 第 6 節在 A_j^Q>0 時把 \|Q\| 截斷質量正規化為空間機率測度，作為後續方向／間隙缺陷的權重。 | Section 6 normalizes truncated \|Q\|-mass to a spatial probability measure whenever A_j^Q>0, which then weights all subsequent direction/gap defects. | C5-E |
| `V_j(x)` | 第 7 節在 S_j≠0 時令 V_j=S_j/\|S_j\| 取值於無跡對稱矩陣單位球面 S⁴，並在 S_j=0 時送入墓地 ∂_S。 | Section 7 sets V_j=S_j/\|S_j\| in the unit sphere S⁴ of trace-free symmetric matrices when S_j≠0, and sends S_j=0 to the cemetery ∂_S. | C5-E |
| `\Xi_j^{S\theta}` | 第 7 節將配對 (應變方向 V_j, 間隙狀態 θ_j=ϑ(S_j)) 對 ν_j^Q 的 push-forward 作為緊緻狀態空間上的 Q 加權缺陷測度。 | Section 7 takes the push-forward of the pair (strain direction V_j, gap state θ_j=ϑ(S_j)) under ν_j^Q as the Q-weighted joint defect measure on the compact state space. | C5-E |
| `\mathfrak{g}_j(\delta)` | 第 8 節記錄 Q 加權測度落在 {θ_j≤δ} 的質量，並以弱極限 Ξ_*^{Sθ} 定義極限間隙質量 𝔤_*(δ)。 | Section 8 records the Q-weighted mass of {θ_j≤δ} and defines the limit gap mass 𝔤_*(δ) from any weak limit Ξ_*^{Sθ}. | C5-E |
| `\mathfrak{G}_*` | 第 9 節取極限測度在 {θ=0} 的質量（等同 δ↓0 時 𝔤_* 的極限），正值時稱中間隙缺陷測度活躍。 | Section 9 takes the limit-measure mass of {θ=0} (equivalently the δ↓0 limit of 𝔤_*), and calls the middle-gap defect measure active when this mass is positive. | C5-E |
| `\kappa_j^Q` | 第 10 節以 Q 方向 U_j=Q_j/\|Q_j\|∈S⁵ 的 ν_j^Q 平均之模長記錄七點抵消極端分支，第 11 節證明其趨於 0 時不能收斂到單一強中間方向。 | Section 10 records the modulus of the ν_j^Q-mean of the Q-direction U_j=Q_j/\|Q_j\|∈S⁵ as the seven-point cancellation extreme branch, and Section 11 proves that κ_j^Q→0 cannot concentrate on a single strong-middle direction. | C5-E |
| `\mathcal{S}_\delta` | 第 12 節定義 {V∈S⁴: ϑ(V)≥δ}，其上 C5-D 錐半徑與半空間裕度可取均勻常數 r_δ、γ_δ ≳ δ。 | Section 12 defines {V∈S⁴: ϑ(V)≥δ}, on which the C5-D cone radius and half-space margin admit uniform constants r_δ, γ_δ ≳ δ. | C5-E |
| `E_S(\eta)` | 第 15 節對 0<η<1 定義 {\|S\|²≥η\|Q\|}，作為把方向洩漏轉成導數庫存前的應變承載分支。 | Section 15 defines {\|S\|²≥η\|Q\|} for 0<η<1, the strain-carrying branch used before converting directional leakage into derivative stock. | C5-E |
| `E_\omega(\eta)` | 第 15–16 節定義 {\|S\|²<η\|Q\|}，並由 \|Q\|≤\|S\|²+c_ω\|ω\|²（c_ω=√2/4）得到其上 \|ω\|² ≳ \|Q\|。 | Sections 15–16 define {\|S\|²<η\|Q\|} and, from \|Q\|≤\|S\|²+c_ω\|ω\|² with c_ω=√2/4, obtain \|ω\|² ≳ \|Q\| there. | C5-E |
| `\mathfrak{H}_R` | 第 20 節將應變承載洩漏經加權 Poincaré 得到的 ∫\|∇S\|² 無量綱化為高階應變波動庫存，並在 E-DER 分支滿足 ℌ_R ≳ a_R^Q。 | Section 20 nondimensionalizes the weighted-Poincaré output ∫\|∇S\|² of strain-carrying leakage into a higher-derivative strain fluctuation stock, which obeys ℌ_R ≳ a_R^Q on the E-DER branch. | C5-E |
| `\mathfrak{W}_R` | 第 20 節將渦量主導洩漏上的 ∫χ\|ω\|² 無量綱化為局部臨界渦量／能散庫存，並在 E-VORT 分支滿足 𝔚_R ≳ a_R^Q。 | Section 20 nondimensionalizes ∫χ\|ω\|² on vorticity-dominant leakage into a local critical vorticity/enstrophy stock, which obeys 𝔚_R ≳ a_R^Q on the E-VORT branch. | C5-E |
| `a_R^Q` | 第 20 節定義無量綱局部 Q 強度 a_R^Q=R ν^{-2} A_χ^Q，作為第 21 節三分法的非退化強度閾值尺度。 | Section 20 defines the dimensionless local Q intensity a_R^Q=R ν^{-2} A_χ^Q, the nondegeneracy scale for the Section 21 trichotomy. | C5-E |
| `M_\delta` | 第 23 節記錄可測集 G_δ={ϑ≤δ} 所承擔的中間源 ∫ λ₂⁺\|S\|²，C5-E.7 由此迫使該集上的三次應變至少以 δ^{-1} 放大。 | Section 23 records the middle source ∫ λ₂⁺\|S\|² carried by G_δ={ϑ≤δ}, from which C5-E.7 forces cubic strain on that set at least at rate δ^{-1}. | C5-E |
| `A_{\mathrm{eff}}(f)` | 第 25 節對非零 f∈L²∩L³ 定義三次／二次範數比，並得到 A_eff(f)≤‖f‖_∞。 | Section 25 defines the cubic-to-quadratic norm ratio of a nonzero f∈L²∩L³, which satisfies A_eff(f)≤‖f‖_∞. | C5-E |
| `V_{\mathrm{eff}}(f)` | 第 26 節定義具體積量綱的比 V_eff(f)=‖f‖₂⁶/‖f‖₃⁶，在振幅大致常數的集合上與該集合體積同階。 | Section 26 defines the volume-dimensional ratio V_eff(f)=‖f‖₂⁶/‖f‖₃⁶, which is comparable to the volume of a set on which f is roughly constant. | C5-E |
| `E_c(f)` | 第 27 節 C5-E.8 定義 \|f\|≥c A_eff(f) 的顯式集合，其體積 ≤ c^{-2} V_eff(f) 且承載至少 1-c 比例的三次活動。 | Section 27 (C5-E.8) defines the explicit set {\|f\|≥c A_eff(f)}, which has volume ≤ c^{-2} V_eff(f) and carries at least a 1-c fraction of the cubic activity. | C5-E |
| `\phi_{S,3}(R)` | 第 29 節在祖先尺度 R 上將 V_eff(S) 除以 R³，C5-E.9 證明非退化中間負荷與有界應變庫存迫使它隨 δ→0 而崩潰。 | Section 29 divides V_eff(S) by the ancestry-scale volume R³, and C5-E.9 proves that nondegenerate middle load plus bounded strain stock forces this ratio to collapse as δ→0. | C5-E |
| `b_R^{\mathrm{mid}}` | 第 29 節定義無量綱局部中間隙負荷 b_R^{mid}=R³ ν^{-3} M_δ，C5-E.9 在其有正下界時迫使有效體積崩潰。 | Section 29 defines the dimensionless local middle-gap load b_R^{mid}=R³ ν^{-3} M_δ, whose positive lower bound in C5-E.9 forces effective-volume collapse. | C5-E |
| `e_R^S` | 第 29–30 節定義 e_R^S=R ν^{-2} ‖S‖₂²，若它無界則中間隙路線改走應變能散逃逸而非有效體積崩潰。 | Sections 29–30 define e_R^S=R ν^{-2} ‖S‖₂², whose unboundedness is the strain-enstrophy-escape alternative to effective-volume collapse on the middle-gap route. | C5-E |
| `r_{\mathrm{sp}}` | 第 31 節取 r_sp ≍ δ_sp^{-1} c^{-2/3} V_eff(S)^{1/3}，使有效超水平集在任意基點具一線方向的一維 δ_sp-稀疏性。 | Section 31 takes r_sp ≍ δ_sp^{-1} c^{-2/3} V_eff(S)^{1/3}, so that the effective superlevel set is 1D δ_sp-sparse along some line through every base point. | C5-E |
| `\Theta_*^{SDef}` | 第 43 節把本輪 Q 加權方向／間隙測度、間隙質量、方向分散、導數庫存、渦量庫存、有效體積比、稀疏半徑與導數定理介面狀態打包為 True-ETN 缺陷狀態。 | Section 43 packages this round's Q-weighted direction/gap measure, gap mass, directional dispersion, derivative stock, vorticity stock, effective-volume ratio, sparseness radius, and derivative-theorem interface status into a True-ETN defect state. | C5-E |
| `\mathbf{Derivative\text{-}Intermittency\ Pre\text{-}Gate}` | 第 0、34 與 36 節將 ℌ_R、𝔚_R、φ_{S,3} 等已證場缺陷命名為導數／間歇前置閘，強調尚未對接已發表的 Grujić–Xu 正則假設。 | Sections 0, 34 and 36 name the proved field defects ℌ_R, 𝔚_R, φ_{S,3} a derivative/intermittency pre-gate, stressing that they do not yet match the published Grujić–Xu regularity hypotheses. | C5-E |
| `Q\Rightarrow\mathrm{Gap}\vee\mathrm{Derivative}\vee\mathrm{Vorticity}` | 第 21 節 C5-E.6 斷言：在非退化局部 Q 強度下，反覆小二次均值必走中間隙缺陷、應變導數波動庫存 ℌ_R≳1、或渦量主導洩漏 𝔚_R≳1 之一。 | Section 21 (C5-E.6) asserts that under nondegenerate local Q intensity, recurrent small quadratic mean must take at least one of middle-gap defect, strain-derivative stock ℌ_R≳1, or vorticity-dominant leakage 𝔚_R≳1. | C5-E |
| `\text{Grujić--Xu 2024, Thm.\ 3.5/3.14}` | 第 1.2 與第 35 節援引 J. Math. Fluid Mech. 26, Article 53 (2024) 的 Theorem 3.5／3.14，其前件是 D^k u 或 D^k ω 的分量／符號超水平集在後續解析時間的一維稀疏性。 | Sections 1.2 and 35 invoke Theorems 3.5 and 3.14 of J. Math. Fluid Mech. 26, Article 53 (2024), whose antecedents are 1D sparseness of component/sign superlevel sets of D^k u or D^k ω at a later analytic time. | C5-E |

## C5-F

| 符號 Symbol | 中文定義 | Definition (EN) | 出處 Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `k_1 \le k_2 \le k_3` | 在第2節中，定義為正規化且跡為零之對稱張量 $K$ 的排序特徵值，並假設 $k_2 \ge 0$。 | Introduced in Section 2 as the ordered eigenvalues of a normalized trace-free symmetric tensor $K$ with $k_2 \ge 0$. | C5-F |
| `\vartheta(K)` | 在第2節中定義的參數，表示第二與第三主特徵值之積，用以量化能隙退化程度。 | Defined in Section 2 as the product of the second and third eigenvalues, quantifying the middle gap degeneration. | C5-F |
| `P_1(K)` | 在第4節中定義，對應於最壓縮特徵值 $k_1$ 的秩一光譜投影子。 | Defined in Section 4 as the rank-one spectral projector corresponding to the most-compressive eigenvalue $k_1$. | C5-F |
| `K_\ast` | 在第6節中指出，當中能隙退化時，應變張量的極限形狀仍保有明確的最壓縮特徵值。 | Identified in Section 6 as the limit strain shape when the middle gap degenerates, retaining a distinct most-compressive eigenvalue. | C5-F |
| `G(e_1)` | 在第0節與第19節中提及，用於計算與遠場壓力主導矩陣交互作用的二次幾何。 | Mentioned in Sections 0 and 19 as the quadratic geometry for evaluating interactions with the far-pressure dominant matrix. | C5-F |
| `H_{e,\delta}` | 在第7節中定義的半空間矩陣，證明固定壓縮軸能將所有局部二次方向推入嚴格半空間。 | Defined in Section 7 as the half-space matrix showing that a fixed compressive axis forces all local quadratic directions into a strict half-space. | C5-F |
| `H_{e,\sigma}` | 在第11節中定義，為放寬至容許壓縮軸在一個窄圓錐帽內變動的半空間條件算子。 | Defined in Section 11 as the half-space condition operator relaxed to allow the compressive axis to vary within a narrow cap. | C5-F |
| `\alpha_\delta` | 在第13節中定義，為使所有 Q 方向能落入同一半空間所允許的最大軸傾角閾值。 | Defined in Section 13 as the maximum axis tilt threshold allowing all Q directions to fall into a common half-space. | C5-F |
| `[e] \in \mathbb{RP}^2` | 在第16節中指出，因正負方向產生相同投影子，故真實的軸狀態存在於緊緻射影平面中。 | Noted in Section 16 that since opposing directions yield the same projector, the true axis state lies in the compact projective plane. | C5-F |
| `\nu_j^{axis}` | 在第17節中定義，為推廣至活動 Q 核心上的壓縮軸機率分佈測度。 | Defined in Section 17 as the probability measure of the compressive axis over the active Q cores. | C5-F |
| `F` | 在第19節中引用的無跡對稱矩陣，代表共同諧和遠場壓力的主導部分。 | Referenced in Section 19 as the trace-free symmetric matrix representing the leading part of the common harmonic far pressure. | C5-F |
| `\Omega_F^-(c)` | 在第19節中定義，為使得壓縮軸能與遠場壓力產生足夠負向補償的射影方向集合。 | Defined in Section 19 as the set of projective directions where the compressive axis yields sufficient negative compensation with the far pressure. | C5-F |
| `(-,+,+)` | 在第20節中分類的一種壓力慣性標記，具備單一負特徵值，會導致軸向鎖定於射影帽內。 | Classified in Section 20 as the pressure inertia signature with a single negative eigenvalue, which forces the axis to lock into a projective cap. | C5-F |
| `(-,-,+)` | 在第20節中分類的另一種壓力慣性標記，具備雙負特徵值，其負二次區域形成一個帶狀而非單一帽區。 | Classified in Section 20 as the pressure inertia signature with two negative eigenvalues, yielding a projective belt rather than a single cap. | C5-F |
| `\alpha_F(c)` | 在第22節中定義的射影帽半徑，由強負壓力補償邊界 $c$ 與特徵值差距所決定。 | Defined in Section 22 as the projective cap radius dictated by the strong negative pressure margin $c$ and the eigenvalue gaps. | C5-F |
| `\mathfrak W_R` | 在第29節中重申，為從 C5-E 繼承而來、在局部核心尺度上的渦度 $L^2$ 物理量。 | Reiterated in Section 29 as the $L^2$ vorticity quantity over the local core scale, inherited from C5-E. | C5-F |
| `P_{st}(\omega\otimes\omega)` | 在第31節與32節中討論，為映射至 Miller 正交算子架構中的擁擠項。 | Discussed in Sections 31 and 32 as the congestion term mapping to the Miller orthogonal operator architecture. | C5-F |
| `P_{st}^{\perp}(\omega\otimes\omega)` | 在第31節與33節中出現，代表無法直接成為實際壓力黑塞矩陣、而需列入正交約束互補空間的剩餘分量。 | Appeared in Sections 31 and 33 representing the residual component forced into the orthogonal constraint complement space, strictly not the actual pressure Hessian. | C5-F |
| `\mathfrak H_R` | 在第34節中引入，導致必定存在達成尺度臨界振幅的二階速度導數點。 | Introduced in Section 34, which forces the existence of points where the second velocity derivative reaches a scale-critical amplitude. | C5-F |
| `\widehat A_S` | 在第37節中定義，用以衡量 NS 縮放座標下的中間能隙三次方有效振幅。 | Defined in Section 37 as the effective cubic amplitude of the middle gap in NS-scaled coordinates. | C5-F |
| `\rho_{\rm dir}` | 在第39節中分析，對應固定 $k=1$ 的情況，其在空間指數上比中能隙間歇性路徑來得差。 | Analyzed in Section 39 for fixed $k=1$, possessing a spatial exponent structurally less favorable than the middle-gap intermittency route. | C5-F |
| `\rho_{\rm chain}^{(k)}` | 在第43節中引用自 Grujić–Xu 定理 3.14 的輔助尺度，具有形式上更佳的空間指數，但受限於高階導數階層假設。 | Referenced in Section 43 from Grujić–Xu Theorem 3.14, having a formally better spatial exponent but constrained by high-order derivative hierarchy hypotheses. | C5-F |
| `k_j^{best}` | 在第44節中定義，為在每次遞迴事件中綜合各項閥值與定理條件所選出的最有利導數階數。 | Defined in Section 44 as the most favorable derivative order selected for each recurrent event based on amplitude, sparsity, and theorem thresholds. | C5-F |
| `\Theta_\ast^{Axis}` | 在第52節中定義的新狀態向量，封裝了壓縮軸分佈、能隙缺陷與遠場壓力標記。 | Defined in Section 52 as the new state vector encapsulating compressive-axis distribution, gap defect, and far-pressure metadata. | C5-F |
| `\mathfrak C_{\rm axis}(\alpha)` | 在第53節中定義，用以測量壓縮軸機率測度在任意半徑為 $\alpha$ 的射影球內的最大質量。 | Defined in Section 53 to measure the maximum mass of the compressive-axis probability measure within any projective ball of radius \alpha. | C5-F |
| `\Theta_\ast^{F}` | 在第56節中定義，為包含軸、壓力與最佳導數階數等多維屬性的更新版 C5-F 真實 ETN 狀態向量。 | Defined in Section 56 as the updated C5-F True ETN state vector encompassing axes, pressures, and optimal derivative orders. | C5-F |
| `c_\ast^P` | 在第52節中作為 C5 共享狀態的一部分引入，代表壓力能有效鎖定軸的負向補償強度。 | Introduced in Section 52 as part of the C5 shared state, representing the negative compensation strength by which pressure effectively locks the axis. | C5-F |

## C5-G

| 符號 Symbol | 中文定義 | Definition (EN) | 出處 Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `\text{Grujić--Xu Theorem 3.5}` | 第1.1節將此定理作為不修改假設的外部閘：若在可容許稍後時刻上選定的 D^ku 分量/符號超水平集在尺度 ρ 上對每個 x_0 呈一維 δ-稀疏，則 T^* 不是爆破時刻。 | Section 1.1 takes this theorem as an unmodified external gate: 1D δ-sparseness of a selected D^ku component/sign superlevel set at scale ρ about every x_0 on an admissible later time implies T^* is not a blow-up time. | C5-G |
| `T^\ast` | 第1.1節將 T^* 作為假設的首次爆破時刻，而定理 3.5 的結論是在空間稀疏條件成立時它不是爆破時刻。 | Section 1.1 treats T^* as a putative first blow-up time, which Theorem 3.5 concludes is not a blow-up time once the spatial sparseness condition holds. | C5-G |
| `s=s(t)` | 第1.1節要求存在稍後時刻 s=s(t) 落在 D^ku 逃逸時刻 t 之後長度約 \\|D^ku(t)\\|_\infty^{-6/(2k+3)} 的明確區間內，閘閉合只能在此窗口上宣告。 | Section 1.1 requires a later time s=s(t) in an explicit interval of length ~\\|D^ku(t)\\|_\infty^{-6/(2k+3)} after the D^ku escape time t; gate closure may be declared only on this window. | C5-G |
| `A_k(s)` | 第4節在預奇異光滑時刻 s<T^* 上將固定階 k≥1 的導數振幅定義為 A_k(s)=\\|D^ku(s)\\|_\infty。 | Section 4 defines the fixed-order amplitude A_k(s)=\\|D^ku(s)\\|_\infty at a pre-singular smooth time s<T^*. | C5-G |
| `L_k(s)` | 第4節在同一預奇異光滑時刻上將固定階 k 的導數 L^2 質量定義為 L_k(s)=\\|D^ku(s)\\|_2。 | Section 4 defines the fixed-order L^2 mass L_k(s)=\\|D^ku(s)\\|_2 at the same pre-singular smooth time. | C5-G |
| `V_{\lambda,k}^{\zeta,i,\pm}` | 第5節對任意多重指標 \|ζ\|=k、分量 i 與符號 ± 定義超水平集 V_{λ,k}^{ζ,i,±}={x:(D^ζ u_i)^±(x)>λ A_k}，且本輪體積界對所有分量/符號一致成立。 | Section 5 defines the component/sign superlevel set V_{λ,k}^{ζ,i,±}={x:(D^ζ u_i)^±(x)>λ A_k} for any multi-index \|ζ\|=k, component i and sign ±, with the volume bound holding uniformly over all such choices. | C5-G |
| `V_k^{\mathrm{eff}}` | 第8節將固定階有效體積定義為具有體積量綱的比 V_k^{eff}=L_k^2/A_k^2。 | Section 8 defines the fixed-order effective volume as the volume-dimension ratio V_k^{eff}=L_k^2/A_k^2. | C5-G |
| `r_{\mathrm{vol},k}` | 第8節在固定定理對 (λ,δ) 後把有效體積轉成一維稀疏尺度 r_{vol,k}=C_{λ,δ} L_k^{2/3} A_k^{-2/3}。 | Section 8 converts effective volume into the 1D sparseness scale r_{vol,k}=C_{λ,δ} L_k^{2/3} A_k^{-2/3} after a theorem pair (λ,δ) is fixed. | C5-G |
| `r_{\mathrm{GX},k}` | 第10節將已發表的定理 3.5 直接目標尺度定義為 r_{GX,k}=1/(2^k c_{GX,k} A_k^{3/(2k+3)})，其中 c_{GX,k}=c(M,\\|u_0\\|_2)。 | Section 10 defines the published Theorem 3.5 direct target scale as r_{GX,k}=1/(2^k c_{GX,k} A_k^{3/(2k+3)}), with c_{GX,k}=c(M,\\|u_0\\|_2). | C5-G |
| `\mathfrak{G}_k^{\mathrm{dir}}` | 第11節將定理就緒的固定階直接閘比定義為 \mathfrak{G}_k^{dir}=r_{vol,k}/r_{GX,k}，並寫成 L_k^{2/3} A_k^{-(4k-3)/(3(2k+3))} 的顯式式。 | Section 11 defines the theorem-ready fixed-order direct gate ratio \mathfrak{G}_k^{dir}=r_{vol,k}/r_{GX,k}, equivalently C_{λ,δ} 2^k c_{GX,k} L_k^{2/3} A_k^{-(4k-3)/(3(2k+3))}. | C5-G |
| `\mathfrak{C}_k^{\mathrm{eff}}` | 第20節將固定階有效濃度指標定義為 \mathfrak{C}_k^{eff}=L_k^2/A_k^{(4k-3)/(2k+3)}，在固定定理歸一化下閘條件即此量不大於 C_{GX,k}。 | Section 20 defines the fixed-order effective concentration index \mathfrak{C}_k^{eff}=L_k^2/A_k^{(4k-3)/(2k+3)}, so that the gate is \mathfrak{C}_k^{eff}\le C_{GX,k} under the theorem normalization. | C5-G |
| `r_{\mathrm{apr},k}` | 第2節記錄定理 3.7 僅由 Leray 能量界得到的固定階先驗體積稀疏尺度 r_{apr,k}∼c(\\|u_0\\|_2) A_k^{-2/(2k+3)}，其指數大於定理 3.5 的 3/(2k+3)，即固定階尺度缺口。 | Section 2 records the Theorem 3.7 a-priori volumetric sparseness scale r_{apr,k}∼c(\\|u_0\\|_2) A_k^{-2/(2k+3)} coming from the Leray energy bound, whose exponent is larger than the Theorem 3.5 exponent 3/(2k+3) and thus encodes the fixed-order scaling gap. | C5-G |
| `\text{volume-to-line lemma}` | 第7節沿用 C3-W 純幾何引理：若可測集 E⊂R^3 滿足 \|E\|<c_3 δ^3 r^3，則對任意基點 x_0 存在方向使 E 在長度 2r 的線段上佔有率 ≤δ。 | Section 7 recalls the C3-W geometric lemma: if a measurable E⊂R^3 satisfies \|E\|<c_3 δ^3 r^3, then for any base point x_0 there is a line direction along which the one-dimensional occupancy of E on the segment of length 2r is ≤δ. | C5-G |
| `\mathrm{COMPSIGN}` | 第16節說明原 C5-A 的 COMPSIGN 缺陷——僅有模長幾何而定理要分量/符號幾何——在 C5-G 直接體積閘中不再是獨立缺陷。 | Section 16 records that the C5-A COMPSIGN defect (magnitude geometry versus the theorem's required component/sign geometry) is no longer an independent defect on the C5-G direct-volume gate. | C5-G |
| `\mathrm{SHELLFULL}` | 第17節說明因本輪直接對完整場 D^ku 估計量而不使用殼層場 u_q，故 SHELLFULL 也不再是固定階直接路線的獨立缺陷。 | Section 17 records that SHELLFULL is no longer an independent fixed-k direct-route defect because the argument estimates the full field D^ku and never uses a shell field u_q. | C5-G |
| `\mathfrak{D}_k^{\mathrm{dir}}` | 第18節將固定階直接路線真正殘餘壓成缺陷族 \mathfrak{D}_k^{dir}={MULT, TIME}，即有效體積/重數缺陷與稍後時刻缺陷。 | Section 18 compresses the true residual of the fixed-order direct route to the defect family \mathfrak{D}_k^{dir}={MULT, TIME}, namely the effective-volume/multiplicity defect and the later-time defect. | C5-G |
| `\mathsf{T}_k` | 第46節以 \mathsf{T}_k∈{0,1} 標記定理 3.5 稍後時刻窗口是否與有利幾何/振幅窗口對齊。 | Section 46 lets \mathsf{T}_k∈{0,1} indicate whether the Theorem 3.5 later-time window aligns with a favorable geometry/amplitude window. | C5-G |
| `\Theta_k^{\mathrm{dir}}` | 第46節將固定階直接缺陷狀態定義為對 Θ_k^{dir}=⟨\mathfrak{G}_k^{dir}, \mathsf{T}_k⟩，假設生存者在每個相關逃逸時刻必須維持閘比>1 或時刻旗標為 0。 | Section 46 defines the fixed-order direct defect state as the pair Θ_k^{dir}=⟨\mathfrak{G}_k^{dir}, \mathsf{T}_k⟩; a hypothetical survivor must recurrently keep the gate ratio >1 or the time flag equal to 0. | C5-G |
| `F\in\operatorname{Sym}_0(3)` | 第23節沿用 C5-F 的無跡對稱共同遠場壓力矩陣 F∈Sym_0(3) 作為壓力簽名狀態的承載物件。 | Section 23 reuses the C5-F traceless symmetric common far-pressure matrix F∈Sym_0(3) as the carrier of pressure-signature state. | C5-G |
| `\widehat{F}` | 第23節在 F≠0 時將其正規化為 \widehat{F}=F/\|F\|_F，落在無跡對稱矩陣的單位球面 S^4∩Sym_0(3) 上。 | Section 23 normalizes a nonzero far-pressure matrix to \widehat{F}=F/\|F\|_F on the unit sphere S^4∩Sym_0(3) of traceless symmetric matrices. | C5-G |
| `\mathcal{S}_{1-}` | 第24節將單壓縮簽名區域定義為開集 \mathcal{S}_{1-}={\widehat{F}: sig F=(-,+,+)}。 | Section 24 defines the single-compression signature region as the open set \mathcal{S}_{1-}={\widehat{F}: sig F=(-,+,+)}. | C5-G |
| `\mathcal{S}_{2-}` | 第24節將雙壓縮簽名區域定義為開集 \mathcal{S}_{2-}={\widehat{F}: sig F=(-,-,+)}。 | Section 24 defines the double-compression signature region as the open set \mathcal{S}_{2-}={\widehat{F}: sig F=(-,-,+)}. | C5-G |
| `\Sigma_P` | 第24節將兩簽名開區域的共同邊界定義為行列式零集 Σ_P={\widehat{F}: det F=0}。 | Section 24 defines the common boundary of the two signature regions as the determinant-zero set Σ_P={\widehat{F}: det F=0}. | C5-G |
| `d_{\mathrm{sig}}(F)` | 第25節將壓力簽名缺陷距離定義為 d_{sig}(F)=dist(\widehat{F},Σ_P)，並在 F=0 時令其為 0，從而使 d_{sig}∈[0,1] 成為緊緻簽名元資料。 | Section 25 defines the pressure-signature defect distance d_{sig}(F)=dist(\widehat{F},Σ_P), set to 0 when F=0, so that d_{sig}∈[0,1] is compact signature metadata. | C5-G |
| `\Delta p=-\|S\|^2+\tfrac12\|\omega\|^2` | 第32節由不可壓縮公式寫出精確壓力 Poisson 恆等式 Δp=-\|S\|^2+(1/2)\|ω\|^2，用以把渦量主導洩漏同步成正壓力曲率。 | Section 32 records the exact incompressible pressure-Poisson identity Δp=-\|S\|^2+(1/2)\|ω\|^2, used to synchronize vorticity-dominant leakage with positive pressure curvature. | C5-G |
| `E_\omega(\eta)` | 第33節回顧 C5-E 的渦量主導集 E_ω(η)={\|S\|^2<η\|Q\|}，作為把洩漏同步到 (Δp)_+ 的空間承載集。 | Section 33 recalls the C5-E vorticity-dominant set E_ω(η)={\|S\|^2<η\|Q\|} as the spatial carrier that synchronizes leakage onto (Δp)_+. | C5-G |
| `r_\eta` | 第33節在 E_ω(η) 上得到點態比 \|S\|^2<r_η\|ω\|^2，其中 r_η=η c_ω/(1-η) 且 c_ω=√2/4。 | Section 33 obtains the pointwise ratio \|S\|^2<r_η\|ω\|^2 on E_ω(η), with r_η=η c_ω/(1-η) and c_ω=√2/4. | C5-G |
| `P_{st}^{\perp}` | 第37節令 P_{st} 為對稱矩陣場到應變約束空間的 L^2 正交投影，並定義補投影 P_{st}^⊥=I-P_{st}。 | Section 37 lets P_{st} be the L^2 orthogonal projection of symmetric-matrix fields onto the strain constraint space and defines the complement projection P_{st}^⊥=I-P_{st}. | C5-G |
| `\nabla^2p=-(C_A+C_S+C_\omega)` | 第39節對應變方程施加 P_{st}^⊥ 得到精確帳本 ∇^2p=-(C_A+C_S+C_ω)，三項分別為平流、應變平方與渦量非線性的約束補。 | Section 39 applies P_{st}^⊥ to the strain equation to obtain the exact ledger ∇^2p=-(C_A+C_S+C_ω), the three terms being the advection, strain-square, and vorticity-nonlinear constraint complements. | C5-G |
| `\Theta_\ast^{\mathrm{DG}}` | 第60節將 C5-G 導數真 ETN 狀態打包為 Θ_*^{DG}=⟨k, A_k, L_k, V_k^{eff}, r_{vol,k}, r_{GX,k}, \mathfrak{G}_k^{dir}, \mathsf{T}_k⟩。 | Section 60 packages the C5-G derivative True-ETN state as Θ_*^{DG}=⟨k, A_k, L_k, V_k^{eff}, r_{vol,k}, r_{GX,k}, \mathfrak{G}_k^{dir}, \mathsf{T}_k⟩. | C5-G |

## C5-H

| 符號 Symbol | 中文定義 | Definition (EN) | 出處 Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `A_k(s)` | 速度場第 k 階空間導數的 L-infinity 範數（定義於第 0 節）。 | The L-infinity norm of the k-th order spatial derivative of the velocity field (Sec 0). | C5-H |
| `L_k(s)` | 速度場第 k 階空間導數的 L2 範數（定義於第 0 節）。 | The L2 norm of the k-th order spatial derivative of the velocity field (Sec 0). | C5-H |
| `r_{vol,k}` | 由 L2 與 L-infinity 範數組合給出的 global volume superlevel set 空間尺度（定義於第 0 節）。 | The spatial scale of the global volume superlevel set derived from L2 and L-infinity norms (Sec 0). | C5-H |
| `r_{dir,k}` | Grujić–Xu Theorem 3.5 所要求的 d=3 fixed-order direct 空間稀疏尺度（定義於第 0 節）。 | The d=3 fixed-order direct spatial sparseness target scale required by Grujić–Xu Theorem 3.5 (Sec 0). | C5-H |
| `\mathfrak{G}_k^{dir}` | 體積半徑與直接目標半徑的比值，若小於等於 1 則觸發 published regularity theorem（定義於第 0 節）。 | The ratio of effective volume radius to the direct target radius, which triggers the regularity theorem if smaller than or equal to 1 (Sec 0). | C5-H |
| `r_{apr,k}` | Theorem 3.7 基於能量層級所給出的先驗 1D 稀疏空間尺度（定義於第 2 節）。 | The a-priori 1D sparseness spatial scale based on energy level given by Theorem 3.7 (Sec 2). | C5-H |
| `r_{chain,k}` | Theorem 3.14 的 asymptotic critical velocity chain 空間目標尺度（定義於第 3 節）。 | The asymptotic critical velocity chain spatial target scale from Theorem 3.14 (Sec 3). | C5-H |
| `\mathcal R(k,c,t)` | Grujić–Xu 用於 ascending / descending chains 比較並進行動態插值的精確正規化振幅（定義於第 4 節）。 | The exact normalized amplitude used by Grujić–Xu to compare ascending/descending chains for dynamic interpolation (Sec 4). | C5-H |
| `\tau_{dir,k}` | Theorem 3.5 直接空間門檻要求的 admissible time window 延遲，帶有指數衰減因子（定義於第 8 節）。 | The admissible time window delay for Theorem 3.5 direct spatial gate, bearing an exponential decay factor (Sec 8). | C5-H |
| `\tau_{chain,k}` | Theorem 3.14 動態鏈機制的時間窗延遲，不帶直接門檻的指數衰減因子（定義於第 9 節）。 | The time window delay for Theorem 3.14 dynamic chain mechanism, which is free of direct-gate exponential decay factors (Sec 9). | C5-H |
| `M_k(t)` | 速度場分佈的第 k 階 L2 旋轉不變頻譜矩（定義於第 10 節）。 | The k-th order L2 rotationally invariant spectral moment of the velocity field (Sec 10). | C5-H |
| `L_k^\sharp` | L2 頻譜矩的平方根，用於控制 fixed-order component 的 L2 norm（定義於第 10 節）。 | The square root of the L2 spectral moment, bounding the L2 norms of fixed-order components (Sec 10). | C5-H |
| `\Lambda_k` | 兩步 L2 頻譜頻率，由 Fourier moment log-convexity 強迫為單調遞增（定義於第 12 節）。 | The two-step L2 spectral frequency, forced to be monotone by Fourier moment log-convexity (Sec 12). | C5-H |
| `V_k^{eff}` | 結合頻譜矩平方根與無窮大範數定義的無因次有效體積（定義於第 14 節）。 | The dimensionless effective volume defined by the root L2 moment and infinity norm (Sec 14). | C5-H |
| `\mathfrak{N}_k` | 量化有效體積內含有多少個頻譜尺寸胞元的物理重數，完全不受 log-convexity 控制（定義於第 14 節）。 | The physical multiplicity quantifying how many spectral-sized cells fit in the effective volume, which is completely uncontrolled by log-convexity (Sec 14). | C5-H |
| `\mathfrak{G}_k^{chain,vol}` | 以全局體積半徑除以 Theorem 3.14 鏈尺度目標定義的體積驗證鏈比值（定義於第 19 節）。 | The volume-certified chain ratio dividing the global volume radius by the Theorem 3.14 chain scale (Sec 19). | C5-H |
| `\mathfrak{X}_k` | 頻譜頻率與振幅鏈根的比值，將空間重數階梯與動態鏈振幅階梯連接（定義於第 20 節）。 | The ratio of spectral frequency to amplitude chain root, connecting the spatial multiplicity ladder with the dynamic chain amplitude ladder (Sec 20). | C5-H |
| `\Theta_k^{H}` | 包含鏈振幅、頻譜頻率與胞元重數，顯示 all-order 狀態本質上至少是高維度的（定義於第 22 節）。 | State vector including chain amplitude, spectral frequency, and cell multiplicity, demonstrating the all-order state is intrinsically multidimensional (Sec 22). | C5-H |
| `\varepsilon_k^{eff}` | 相對先驗能量尺度的幾何指數增益，必須大於理論指數缺陷方能跨越門檻（定義於第 29 節）。 | The actual concentration gain exponent relative to the a-priori scale, which must strictly beat the theoretical exponent gap (Sec 29). | C5-H |
| `\text{H-SPEC}` | 頻譜頻率相較振幅鏈根過小的高階缺陷型態（定義於第 37 節）。 | High-order defect where the spectral frequency is insufficient relative to the amplitude chain root (Sec 37). | C5-H |
| `\text{H-MULT}` | 物理重數過大導致全局空間體積無法達到 1D sparseness 尺度的高階缺陷（定義於第 37 節）。 | High-order defect where the physical multiplicity is too large to allow global volume to reach the 1D sparseness scale (Sec 37). | C5-H |
| `\text{H-SAT}` | 高階空間集中度無法比先驗能量稀疏尺度好上任何固定指數冪次的現象（定義於第 32 節）。 | The phenomenon where high-order actual spatial concentration does not improve upon the a-priori sparseness by any fixed exponential power (Sec 32). | C5-H |
| `\text{H-TIME}` | 有利的空間集中發生時間未能對齊 Theorem 3.14 動態容許時間窗的缺陷（定義於第 37 節）。 | Defect where the favorable spatial geometry fails to align with the dynamic theorem-admissible later time window (Sec 37). | C5-H |
| `\text{H-CHAIN}` | 未能滿足遞增/遞減鏈或 Type-A/B 截面分佈的高階結構缺陷（定義於第 37 節）。 | Defect where ascending/descending chains or Type-A/B section topological conditions are not structurally met (Sec 37). | C5-H |
| `\Theta_i^{block}` | 在指數分離的導數區塊上追蹤最大值與各項幾何時間元數據的壓縮狀態向量（定義於第 39 節）。 | Compressed state vector tracking maxima and metadata on exponentially separated derivative blocks (Sec 39). | C5-H |
| `Z_{j,i}` | 映射至 [0,1] 以供全無限階序空間抽取 sectionwise 缺陷測度之正規化向量（定義於第 50 節）。 | Normalized vector compactified into [0,1] for extracting sectionwise defect measures over the infinite order space (Sec 50). | C5-H |
| `\Theta^{H}_{j,i}` | 更新後的 C5 全階狀態張量，正式包含動態鏈狀態與 sign geometry 缺陷追蹤（定義於第 56 節）。 | The updated C5 all-order state tensor formally including dynamic chain states and sign geometry defect tracking (Sec 56). | C5-H |

## C5-I

| 符號 Symbol | 中文定義 | Definition (EN) | 出處 Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `\mathcal{R}(k,c,t)` | 第1.1節引入的Grujić–Xu正規化導數根振幅，用以在固定區段正規化常數c下比較各階導數的相對大小。 | The Grujić–Xu normalized derivative-root amplitude introduced in §1.1, used to compare derivative orders under a fixed section normalization constant c. | C5-I |
| `\ell_{i+1}=\phi(\ell_i),\quad\phi(x)\ge 2x` | 第1.1節以指數分離的導數階區間[ℓ_i,ℓ_{i+1}]與增長函數φ(x)≥2x將導數階切成sections。 | Exponentially separated derivative-order intervals [ℓ_i,ℓ_{i+1}] with growth φ(x)≥2x, introduced in §1.1 to partition derivative orders into sections. | C5-I |
| `m_i` | 第1.1節在每個區段[ℓ_i,ℓ_{i+1}]上選取使R(j,c(ℓ_i),t)達到最大的階m_i。 | The order m_i in each section [ℓ_i,ℓ_{i+1}] that maximises R(j,c(ℓ_i),t), selected in §1.1. | C5-I |
| `\text{Type-\mathcal{A}}` | 第2節稱區段為Type-A，若存在更高階k_i>ℓ_{i+1}使其正規化振幅追上或超越當前區段極大值。 | A section is Type-A, as defined in §2, if some higher order k_i>ℓ_{i+1} eventually catches or overtakes the current section maximum. | C5-I |
| `\text{Type-\mathcal{B}}` | 第2節稱區段為Type-B，若當前區段極大值嚴格支配整個更高階尾部。 | A section is Type-B, as defined in §2, if the current section maximum strictly dominates the entire higher-order tail. | C5-I |
| `r_k(s)` | 第3節定義Theorem 3.14可容許的最大空間檢驗半徑，作為符號幾何與下降引理的鏈尺度。 | The maximal spatially admissible inspection radius of Theorem 3.14, defined in §3 and used as the chain scale for sign geometry and the descent lemma. | C5-I |
| `\widetilde{\mathcal{C}}_k` | 第3節與第24節中依賴初值、區段與階的published常數，同時控制鏈半徑與定理時間窗。 | The published constant depending on initial data, section, and order, appearing in §3 and §24, that controls both the chain radius and the theorem time window. | C5-I |
| `V_{\lambda,k}^{j,\pm}(s)` | 第3節定義選定分量與符號的超位集，作為1D弦佔有率與調和測度檢驗的載體。 | The selected-component/sign superlevel set defined in §3, serving as the carrier of 1D chord occupancy and harmonic-measure tests. | C5-I |
| `A_k(s)` | 第3節以A_k(s)=\\|D^ku(s)\\|_\infty記第k階導數的空間上確界振幅。 | The spatial supremum amplitude of the k-th derivative, defined in §3 by A_k(s)=\\|D^ku(s)\\|_\infty. | C5-I |
| `(\lambda,\delta)` | 第4節給出與調和測度條件一致選取的published參數對，並要求1/(1+λ)<δ<1。 | The published parameter pair of §4, chosen consistently with the harmonic-measure condition and required to satisfy 1/(1+λ)<δ<1. | C5-I |
| `\kappa_{\lambda,\delta}` | 第4節與第22節定義由參數對誘導的正裕度，作為符號厚弦下降引理的係數。 | The positive margin induced by the theorem parameter pair, defined in §4 and §22, which appears as the coefficient in the sign-thick chord descent lemma. | C5-I |
| `b_E(x_0,r,[\nu])` | 第5節定義可測集E沿過x_0、方向[ν]、半徑r的單位化1D弦佔有率，取值於[0,1]。 | The normalized 1D chord occupancy of a measurable set E through x_0 in projective direction [ν] at radius r, defined in §5 and valued in [0,1]. | C5-I |
| `\beta_E(x_0,r)` | 第6節取所有射影方向上弦佔有率的下確界；β_E≤δ等價於存在定理可用的稀疏方向。 | The infimum of chord occupancies over all projective directions, defined in §6; β_E≤δ is equivalent to existence of a theorem-usable sparse direction. | C5-I |
| `\mathsf{SG}_k(s)` | 第7節以二值指標SG_k(s)=1表示level k在時間s對所有基點皆存在半徑≤r_k的δ-稀疏方向，此即Theorem 3.14的真正空間通過。 | The binary indicator of §7, with SG_k(s)=1 iff every basepoint at time s admits a δ-sparse direction at some radius ≤r_k; this is the genuine spatial pass of Theorem 3.14. | C5-I |
| `b_k([\nu])` | 第9節在壞見證點x_k與最大鏈半徑r_k上記錄各方向的弦佔有率，得到取值於(δ,1]的L^∞(RP^2)函數。 | The L^∞(RP^2) function of §9 recording chord occupancy in every direction at the bad witness x_k and maximal chain radius r_k, with values strictly above δ. | C5-I |
| `h(\beta)` | 第12節以Solynin極值估計把線上主動集佔有率β映成調和測度下界，且h嚴格遞減。 | The strictly decreasing Solynin extremal lower bound of §12 converting line active-set occupancy β into a harmonic-measure lower bound. | C5-I |
| `d_k(c)` | 第25節將鏈尺度振幅下降改寫成相鄰正規化根振幅的乘子，並含階乘與定理常數修正。 | The adjacent-order multiplier of §25 converting chain-scale amplitude descent into a comparison of normalized roots, including factorial and theorem-constant corrections. | C5-I |
| `\Phi_k^{bad}` | 第17節定義壞核球B_{r_k}(x_k)內第k階導數L^2質量佔全局L_k^2的分數。 | The fraction of global k-th derivative L^2 mass sitting in the bad-core ball B_{r_k}(x_k), defined in §17. | C5-I |
| `V_k^{eff}` | 第17–18節沿用C5-H有效體積V_k^{eff}=L_k^2/A_k^2，並用以把壞核分數改寫成r_k^3/V_k^{eff}。 | The C5-H effective volume V_k^{eff}=L_k^2/A_k^2, reused in §17–18 to rewrite the bad-core fraction as a multiple of r_k^3/V_k^{eff}. | C5-I |
| `\mathfrak{N}_k` | 第18節沿用C5-H譜胞重數，說明巨大重數可使單個符號厚核只承擔很小的全局導數質量分數。 | The C5-H spectral-cell multiplicity reused in §18, showing that a large multiplicity can make a single sign-thick core carry only a small global derivative-mass fraction. | C5-I |
| `N_k` | 第19節以同一level/time兩兩不交壞球的個數為壞核重數，並由有效體積對r_k^3的比上界控制。 | The number of pairwise disjoint bad balls at a fixed level and time, introduced in §19 and bounded by a multiple of V_k^{eff}/r_k^3. | C5-I |
| `\mu_i^{SG}` | 第37節把區段內各階的二值幾何失敗指標平均成[0,1]上的子概率測度，用以緊緻化符號缺陷在階空間的分佈。 | The subprobability measure on [0,1] of §37 averaging binary geometry-failure indicators along a section, used to compactify the order-space distribution of sign defects. | C5-I |
| `\tau_k` | 第43節把Theorem 3.14的後期時間窗正規化到[1/4,1]，無對齊定理時間時則取值於墳場點∂_T。 | The normalized Theorem 3.14 later-time coordinate of §43, valued in [1/4,1] when an admissible evaluation exists and at the cemetery point ∂_T otherwise. | C5-I |
| `\Theta^{I}` | 第57節把鏈根、型態、空間通過、正規化時間、佔有率、角向剖面、壞核質量、譜胞重數與壞階位置打包成可子列緊緻化的區塊狀態。 | The block/level state of §57 packing chain roots, type, spatial pass, normalized time, occupancy, angular profile, bad-core mass, multiplicity, and bad-order location into a subsequentially compactifiable object. | C5-I |
| `A_{k-1}\ge\kappa_{\lambda,\delta} r_k A_k` | 第22節的C5-I.3：在壞見證點沿選定座標線積分同號厚弦，強迫低一階振幅至少為裕度乘上r_k A_k。 | C5-I.3 of §22: integrating a same-sign thick chord through a bad witness along a selected coordinate line forces the lower-order amplitude to be at least the occupancy margin times r_k A_k. | C5-I |
| `\mathsf{SG}_k(s)=1\ \vee\ \mathcal{R}(k-1,c,s)\ge d_k(c)\mathcal{R}(k,c,s)` | 第27節的C5-I.4：在定理可容許時間，每個k≥1要嘛空間幾何通過，要嘛正規化根振幅付出下降係數d_k的toll。 | C5-I.4 of §27: at a theorem-admissible time, every k≥1 either spatially passes or pays a descending-root toll with coefficient d_k. | C5-I |
| `\frac{\mathcal{R}(K,c,s)}{\mathcal{R}(J,c,s)}>\prod_{n=J+1}^{K}d_n(c)^{-1}\ \Rightarrow\ \exists n\in(J,K]:\mathsf{SG}_n(s)=1` | 第30節的C5-I.5：同一時間上若區間[J,K]的上升增益超過下降係數倒數之積，則該區間不能全部空間失敗，至少一階必須調和通過。 | C5-I.5 of §30: a same-time ascent gain on [J,K] larger than the product of inverse descent coefficients cannot have every intermediate level spatially bad, so at least one harmonic-pass level must puncture the interval. | C5-I |
| `\beta_\ast=\delta` | 第11節與第34節稱反覆壞核的最佳佔有率弱極限恰等於定理閾值δ的情形為調和臨界飽和，對應I-SGCRIT而非有限階通過。 | The I-SGCRIT regime of §11 and §34 in which recurrent bad-core best occupancies weak-limit exactly to the theorem threshold δ; this is a boundary saturation of finite-level failures, not a finite-level pass. | C5-I |
| `\mathsf{Z}_\ast\in\{A_G,A_B,B_G,B_B,\mathrm{TIME}\}` | 第40–41節把每個區段標成Type-A/B與GOOD/BAD的有限字母，另加無對齊定理時間的TIME墳場，從而使無窮區段列具有最終常值聯合型態。 | The finite alphabet of §40–41 labelling each section by Type-A/B and GOOD/BAD, plus a TIME cemetery when no theorem-admissible evaluation can be aligned, so that infinite section sequences admit eventually constant joint types. | C5-I |
| `\chi_k([\nu],s)` | 第62節在正規化弦s∈[-1,1]上追蹤選定高位集的指示函數，作為下一輪C5-J的主要對象。 | The indicator of the selected high set along the normalized chord s∈[-1,1], introduced in §62 as the primary object of the next round C5-J. | C5-I |

| `A_k` | 第0節回顧 C5-I 引入的選定高階導數在空間失效點附近的連鎖尺度上限。 | Bounding supremum of the selected high-order derivative at the chain scale introduced in C5-I, reviewed in Section 0. | C5-J |
| `\kappa_{\lambda,\delta}` | 第8節定義的降階積分懲罰乘子，僅依賴於符號一致高值集的總長度。 | The lower-order descent toll multiplier defined in Section 8, depending only on the total length of the same-sign high set. | C5-J |
